general LLL approximation guarantee does not prove such a norm; the tensor
coefficient law is structured, so generic random-matrix claims cannot be
imported without proof. Enlarging the toy modulus or searching until a key
recovery filter fails is not a replacement for that family theorem.

These trials do not prove a general no-go theorem for dense two-sided public
builders. They do establish that the recorded solver/parameters are not the
requested secure correct FHE construction. No extra hardness assumption or
acyclic key scheduling was introduced, and the goal is not complete.

Verification: both Lean roots build successfully (9,245 jobs), and the expanded
GSWSelfKeyAudit exits successfully with 895 declarations, using only propext,
Classical.choice and Quot.sound. The final logs contain no Lean errors,
warnings or sorryAx. All 469 selected project Lean/configuration files
byte-match the validated container mirror. The final executable trial exits
successfully, its JSON counts and invariants are checked, Python syntax
validation passes, and git diff --check passes. The reference collection
remains unchanged at 39 papers. No sub-agents were used.

Rethinking after the signed-source extraction barrier, 2026-10-10
===============================================================

The latest request is to reconsider the remaining ways, not to regard the
previous unsuccessful parameters as a universal impossibility result. This
review finds a stronger obstruction to the particular public short-preimage
interface. It changes which construction work is worth pursuing. The original
ordinary-LWE-only, unchanged-secret, repeated-refresh objective is unfinished.

A public signed-source transformation
------------------------------------
Start with a binary-secret LWE batch in dimension n:

  y = A^T*t + e,    t in {0,1}^n.

For each column independently sample c in {0,1}. Form the public (n+1)-row
matrix M by adding a body row above the original mask rows:

  M[0,j] = 2*y[j] - sum_i A[i,j] + c[j],
  M[i+1,j] = A[i,j].

The hidden vector v=(1,1-2*t_0,...,1-2*t_(n-1)) satisfies

  v_i^2 = 1 for EVERY coordinate,    v^T*M = 2*e+c.

Neither t nor e is used to form M. Split the columns into M1,M2. The relation
holds for both blocks, with errors e1'=2*e1+c1 and e2'=2*e2+c2. If the original
entrywise radii are E1,E2, the new radii are at most 2*E1+1,2*E2+1.

The parity coins are essential at an even modulus. Conditional on a uniform
body y[j] and any fixed original mask column, 2*y[j]+c[j] is EXACTLY uniform
in Z_q when q is even: each output has two equally likely preimages among
the 2*q input pairs. At odd q multiplication by two is already a permutation,
and adding an independent coin preserves uniformity. Subtracting the mask
sum is a translation. Hence the uniform source branch produces an EXACTLY
uniform M, including independent columns and independent split blocks.
Without c, the top row at an even modulus has a public parity restriction.
There is no claimed reduction to unrandomized signed-secret LWE.

Why free diagonals do not rescue a useful short certificate
----------------------------------------------------------
Suppose a public builder returns D_i for the anchor-pair target w*E_(i+1,0),
with an exact diagonal-relaxed certificate

  P_i = M1*D_i*M2^T
      = w*E_(i+1,0) + diag(P_i) + U_i-U_i^T.

This is the positive product target corresponding to the native negative
quadratic phase. Evaluate the universal matrix identity on v. Alternating
terms vanish, and EVERY diagonal contribution equals its coefficient because
v_i^2=1. The result is

  e1'^T*D_i*e2' = w*(1-2*t_i) + trace(P_i),
  trace(P_i)+w = 2*w*t_i + e1'^T*D_i*e2'.

The left-hand side is publicly computable from M1,M2,D_i. If ||D_i||_1<=L,
its residual has centered magnitude at most

  B = (2*E1+1)*(2*E2+1)*L.

The existing nearest-center decoder recovers t_i whenever

  2*B < centeredDistance(0,2*w).

For the present bit gadget at q=2^levels with levels>=2, choose its q/4
weight. Then 2*w=q/2 and the condition is B<q/4. Applying the builder to all
anchor pairs recovers the ENTIRE original n-bit source key. This does not
require sparse support, small norms for M1*D_i, a public trapdoor, knowledge
of the hidden alternating witnesses, or unpredictable individual secret bits.
A witness can be computed and its certificate checked from the public product.

The same argument applies to a dense one-sided public B_i with product
B_i*M^T and the same free-diagonal certificate. The trace identity has
residual v^T*B_i*(2*e+c), bounded by (2*E+1)*||B_i||_1 since |v_i|=1.
The one-sided trace identity is checked in Lean; this particular l1 corollary
and the full computational reduction below are mathematical deductions here.
Full prescribed-diagonal certificates are included as a restriction. With
zero diagonal their trace is zero; at the q/4 target and the useful residual
margin the signed source cannot have even one such short certificate.

A finite decision reduction, not just a search-security assertion
----------------------------------------------------------------
Reserve one independent verification sample from the ORIGINAL binary LWE
challenge. Let alpha be the builder's joint probability of passing the exact
public certificate and norm checks on the uniform lifted matrix. The tester:

  1. Builds M using public challenge data and fresh parity coins.
  2. Runs the public builder and verifies every needed certificate and norm.
  3. On any failure, accepts the REAL-LWE hypothesis.
  4. On success, extracts all bits from traces and accepts that hypothesis
     exactly when the held-out body minus the extracted-key inner product
     lies in the prescribed source-error window.

With bounded real source errors and the stated margin, real acceptance is
one: either the checks fail, or extraction is correct and verification passes.
In the uniform branch the held-out body is independent uniform even after
conditioning on all training data, builder coins and extracted bits. Its
acceptance probability is at most eta=min(1,(2*E_verify+1)/q), using the
existing card_verification_window_le/uniform_verification_acceptance_le.
Therefore uniform acceptance is at most 1-alpha+alpha*eta and the decision
advantage is at least alpha*(1-eta). A source-error bad event of probability
beta subtracts at most beta. This handles builders that fail on most real
programmed matrices; it does not assume a negligible real-branch failure rate.

Thus high uniform-matrix success and useful publicly verifiable shortness
contradict decisional binary-source LWE under these conditions. To state this
as an ordinary-LWE consequence, the SAME required binary-source hardness
must be derived with its entropy/noise losses and polynomial sample budget.
The reduction uses n source secret bits for an (n+1)-row builder, plus one
verification sample; these dimension/sample changes and negligible losses
must be included in a family statement. No independent binary-LWE assumption
is being added. The existing ordinary-to-binary source route provides the
relevant interface, but the complete new reduction has NOT been formalized.

The useful asymptotic families proposed earlier have polynomial dimensions
and norm budgets, exponentially smaller source noise than the modulus, and
bit-gadget q/4 columns. Their hoped-for public exact-certificate builder falls
under this barrier IF the corresponding ordinary-derived binary source is
hard with negligible losses. The old conditional parameter theorems remain
valid; they do not establish that their builder hypotheses can be realized.
This is a scoped obstruction, not an unconditional impossibility theorem for
all ciphertext formats, all public samplers, or all same-secret GSW designs.

Checked implementation and finite evidence
------------------------------------------
GSWPublicPreimageBarrier.lean proves:

* signedSecret_square and signed_lift_relation over any commutative ring;
* the unit-square diagonal/trace and anchor-target identities;
* trace_relation for dense two-sided free-diagonal certificates;
* one_sided_trace_relation, with no row-support hypothesis;
* the modular bilinear l1 bound and the lifted-error bound;
* recoverBit_of_certificate and recover_lifted_sources, using only public
  matrices and preimages in the actual extractor.

The distributional uniformization argument and finite decision reduction are
mathematical derivations above, rather than new fully formal game theorems.
They are distinguished from the checked pointwise extraction core. No new
security premise, sorry or custom axiom has been inserted.

scripts/check-gsw-public-preimage-barrier.py reuses the public dense LLL/Babai
solver, with no key/error inputs and no source resampling. Eight fixed cases
all solve their requested relaxed systems, checking 24 exact certificates,
24 trace identities and the signed source relation. Seventeen bits meet the
worst-case recovery margin and ALL seventeen are recovered. Twenty-two of
24 observed guesses are correct overall; unsupported guesses in failed-margin
cases do not strengthen the theorem. All eight keys have nonzero Hamming
weight. The source-noise radii are 1,64,32768 at q=2^12,2^16,2^24;
these are diagnostics, not an asymptotic cryptographic parameter family.
Exact finite enumeration additionally checks two input preimages per output
for the uniformization map at moduli 2,4,8,16,32,64,3,5,17 and exhibits the
parity defect when coins are omitted at modulus 16. Results are retained in
GSWPublicPreimageBarrierExploration.json.

Remaining ways after this reconsideration
----------------------------------------
1. Public transcript compilers whose correctness is tied to the SAMPLED key.
   GSWTranscriptCompiler already supplies the complete ordinary-source game
   reduction for arbitrary public H(A,y),C(A,y). The missing construction must
   make C+s^T*H-F(s) small on its real joint source law without requiring a
   universally valid, useful short quadratic matrix certificate. Merely
   letting an exact-certificate D builder inspect extra public bodies is not
   a demonstrated escape: such auxiliary bodies can be generated under an
   independent known binary key at the programmed matrix. A genuinely
   different sampled-law phase construction and its efficient tail/refresh
   invariant are needed. No such algorithm has been supplied in this turn.

2. Private correlations with simulation of the published marginal.
   The private gadget-trapdoor construction already has useful short witnesses
   and finite native correctness. The trace extractor needs the private
   witnesses/product, so it does not automatically attack that published view.
   The missing theorem is a simulator or direct game reduction for all public
   masked controls and retained data at an ordinary challenge secret. Matrix
   marginal uniformity alone is insufficient. Flooding away every residual
   returns conventional native GSW controls and the unresolved circular-view
   obligation; a useful simulator must account for the helpful joint law.

3. A direct augmented plaintext-game proof for the existing native scheme.
   It remains legitimate to retain real controls and prove only the encryption
   challenge hop, using GSWStoredSecurityView/GSWMixedStoredView. Replacing
   every control by independent uniform data is not a necessary proof target.
   The reduction must nevertheless generate the entire real correlated view
   at the unknown challenge secret. No honest-control oracle, unexplained KDM
   advantage, or independent leakage assumption closes that requirement.

4. A distribution-restricted ciphertext-format cycle under the same secret.
   The universal conversion obstruction does not exclude public conversions
   on reachable ciphertext distributions with jointly secure auxiliary data.
   A full cycle must convert to a format with a proved evaluation/refresh
   method and back to native GSW, with closed noise/reachability margins.
   An affine-KDM or randomized-encoding encryption theorem alone supplies
   neither leg. The current reference audits do not provide such a cycle.

Decision: deprioritize further norm tuning of the public exact-certificate
builders in the compatible hard-source regime. The most concrete remaining
interfaces are sampled-law public compilation and private published-marginal
simulation; both need a new mathematical construction/reduction, not another
conditional correctness wrapper. The direct plaintext game and format cycle
remain distinct research targets, not already verified solutions. This turn
narrows the landscape and proves an extraction core; it does not complete the
requested same-secret bootstrappable-GSW security theorem.

Verification: both Lean roots build successfully (9,246 jobs). The expanded
GSWSelfKeyAudit checks 905 declarations, with only propext, Classical.choice
and Quot.sound; module/build/audit logs contain no Lean errors, warnings or
sorryAx. All 470 selected authoritative Lean/configuration files byte-match
the validated container mirror. The new executable exits successfully, its
JSON counts and guaranteed recovery assertions are checked, Python syntax
validation and git diff --check pass. The reference collection is unchanged
at 39 papers. No sub-agents were used.

Trying an ordinary-source rotation of the private gadget, 2026-10-10
=================================================================

The preceding turn is progress: it added a checked extraction barrier that
changes the priority of the public exact-certificate route. This continuation
tries to address the PRIVATE source-simulation gap directly. It implements a
new source embedding and discovers a native-noise obstruction, rather than
assuming that the private trapdoor law is secure. The full goal is unfinished.

A simulator with an available gadget-source body
-----------------------------------------------
Take two ordinary same-secret source blocks

  y0=A0^T*s+e0,    yH=H^T*s+eH,

where H is square and invertible modulo q. Use a rotated gadget H*G0 and an
independent private binary R:

  A=[A0 | H*G0-A0*R],    T=[R;I],    A*T=H*G0,
  y=[y0 ; G0^T*yH-R^T*y0].

Everything in this source transformation is computable from the challenge
transcripts and R, without the unknown secret or either source error. Unlike
the original fixed-gadget programming attempt, its gadget-source body is
available. The exact error of the new second block is

  G0^T*eH-R^T*e0.

It is CORRELATED and may be very large; it is not a fresh ordinary narrow
error vector. Moreover T^T*y=G0^T*yH and T^T*e=G0^T*eH. This experiment does
not relabel that derived error as an ordinary-LWE sample.

For a native off-diagonal target Q, let J=H^-1 and signed/unsigned gadget
coefficients D satisfy D*G0^T=Q*J^T. Set B=D*T^T. Then

  B*A^T=Q,    B*y=Q*J^T*yH.

With binary gadget digits D and binary R, ||B||_1 is polynomial even when
entries of J are large. The actual correction, however, is INDEPENDENT of R
and the private trapdoor: it is the public effective inverse correction on
the selected H source block. The exact native control residual is

  delta = s^T*Q*J^T*eH.

Thus the source-simulation idea succeeds algebraically, and the proposed
private published marginal is explicitly eliminated into a source compiler.
Its actual error has not acquired a useful bound from the short private B.
The derived-source errors, rather than the original source errors, multiply B.

The effective compiler is ell_j+Q_j*J^T*yH with body correction zero. Retaining
the ordinary source blocks as the encryption public key makes this a public
instance of the existing GSWTranscriptCompiler interface. Its established
complete stored-game source bound does not require a KDM replacement term.
This observation does not prove an asymptotically secure correct parameter
family: the native correctness error below fails. A separate new complete
asymptotic implementation/game theorem for this rotated sampler is not claimed.

An invertible H can be selected from a fixed pool using only source masks.
The executable samples 64 entire candidate blocks once and selects the first
invertible block, recording failure if none exists. It never resamples noise,
keys or failed controls. In an asymptotic construction the pool size would
need to grow to make selection failure negligible; a fixed 64 is only a finite
trial choice. All candidate blocks remain in the retained ordinary source.

Why the canonical correction cannot meet the native control bound
---------------------------------------------------------------
Write eta=J^T*eH. For Q=-2^level*E_ij, an active bit s_i=1 has actual residual

  -2^level*eta_j.

At q=2^levels, EVERY nonzero eta_j has a gadget level with
2^level*eta_j=q/2 modulo q. Write eta_j's nonzero canonical representative as
2^k*m with m odd and k<levels; level=levels-1-k gives the half turn. This is
not merely a parity defect of the highest gadget column.

If the fresh error radius is E, that column's centered control noise is at
least q/2-E. In particular it cannot be below q/4 when E<q/4. For a binary key
with at least two active bits, every coordinate j has an active i different
from j. Any nonzero eH therefore yields such an active control obstruction,
because J is invertible and eta=0 iff eH=0. Larger q does not remove the
canonical construction's problem: another binary gadget level reaches q/2.

GSWRotatedTrapdoorSource.lean checks the source/trapdoor and body-projection
identities, the derived error, the rotated certificate, elimination into the
effective correction, and the actual residual. It also proves
exists_half_turn, exists_maximal_centered_error, perturbation_lower_bound,
and exists_noisy_gadget_obstruction. These latter statements establish the
power-of-two obstruction even after adding fresh bounded errors.

The obstruction concerns this canonical inverse-based correction and the
established full native control invariant. It is not a theorem that every
private sampler or every alternative correlated-error refresh invariant fails.
In particular it does not rule out a different transcript-dependent choice
of alternating coefficients with a proved small ACTUAL residual. Such a rule
is currently missing; a private rule requiring the unknown secret/errors
would reintroduce the source-simulation gap.

A public basis change to absorb eta is not an automatic remedy. If the same
H,yH are retained and eta is published, the source key is recovered exactly
as s=J^T*yH-eta. Hiding the source block avoids that specific disclosure, but
then the proposed source simulator still has to generate the error-dependent
public basis without knowing eH. The claimed ordinary-source transformation
above does not provide that operation. No basis-change fix is adopted.

Finite executable evidence
--------------------------
Run PYTHONPATH=/tmp/fhe-preimage-packages python3
scripts/try-gsw-rotated-trapdoor-source.py --output
docs/GSWRotatedTrapdoorSourceExploration.json. The eight fixed geometries are
(n,levels,E)=(2,16,0),(2,16,1),(2,16,64),(3,24,0),(3,24,1),(3,24,64),
(4,24,1),(4,24,64), seeds 12001..12008. Each is tested with explicit fixed
all-one and single-one diagnostic keys. They are not presented as random
cryptographic key draws. All matrices/errors are sampled once; both key modes
use the same selected source geometry/errors, with bodies computed for that
mode's unchanged secret. Every source pool has an invertible selection.

All 2,208 target certificates and 4,224 full-column phase identities pass.
The private l1 maxima range from 1,057 to 5,662 and meet the stated structural
bound n*levels*(m0+1), m0=(n+1)*levels+80. Corrections also agree EXACTLY with
Q*J^T*yH, and every nonzero affected inverse-error coordinate has an explicitly
checked half-turn level. The source errors in all twelve positive-radius cases
are nonzero. Their 52 active half-turn witnesses imply a control-noise lower
bound q/2-E, checked against the actual observed maximum in every case.

Each case performs three refresh cycles on two branches, including two
NAND-and-refresh continuations using the SAME key and controls. Across the
96 calls, 74 selected bits happen to decode correctly, 29 full output margins
pass and 28 sufficient next-NAND-and-rounding margins pass. These numbers
must be separated by the source-noise boundary:

  Zero-radius boundary: 24/24 selected bits, input rounding margins, full
  output margins and next-NAND margins pass. But the selected public H,yH
  reveal s exactly when eH=0. This does not meet the source-hardness premise.

  Positive-radius trials: 50/72 selected bits, 27/72 input margins, 5/72 full
  output margins and 4/72 next-NAND margins pass. Controls have errors near
  q/2 even at radius ONE. Isolated output successes do not restore the native
  invariant or supply a reusable asymptotic correctness/security family.

Thus the attempt supplies a computable source simulation but no secure correct
bootstrapper. It is not enough to say that private preimages are polynomially
short while omitting the amplified source errors.

Further reference and format-cycle audit
---------------------------------------
A fresh primary-source search revalidated the already checked WHT26 and
arXiv:2604.23490v2 claims. The latter explicitly uses layered fresh keys; it
does not satisfy the unchanged-secret restriction. No result is inferred
from their circular-security-free abstracts.
https://link.springer.com/article/10.1186/s42400-025-00431-z
https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23490v2

Downloaded and catalogued PS16, Peikert--Shiehian, into refs/gsw/PS16.pdf.
Section 1.1 conditions unbounded evaluation on circular security. Sections
3.1--3.2 maintain commitment randomness and carry prior error through key
extension. The first scheme supplies no proved reusable same-secret refresh
cycle. Primary citation: https://eprint.iacr.org/2016/196
The 19-page manuscript is dated August 24, 2016; its mirror retrieval has the
same provenance limitation as recorded in the catalog.

Encrypting ciphertext coins instead of secret-key bits remains an interesting
FORMAT idea, not an established completion. For C=P*R+mu*G, multiplication
with G*D(C2)=C2 gives an exact opening

  R_product=R1*D(C2)+mu1*R2.

A commitment/coin metadata format can track such openings. But a refresh
computed from encrypted opening metadata itself uses those metadata
ciphertexts' encryption coins. A reusable design must close that dependency,
prove the entire joint metadata law from ordinary LWE, and maintain a bounded
opening/noise/size invariant after another refresh. An unproved randomness
cycle or a growing tower of encrypted metadata does not meet the objective.
The present audit supplies neither such a cycle nor an impossibility theorem.

Completion audit and next mathematical requirement
-------------------------------------------------
Ordinary-source simulation is concrete for the canonical rotated correction;
its intended native noise/refresh invariant fails. Zero rotation error repairs
correctness while violating the source premise. No one sampler/parameter
family currently satisfies both halves. Neither the reference audit nor the
new conditional source identities prove the requested complete security result.

The remaining constructive target is a transcript-dependent actual-residual
rule or a private full-marginal simulator that preserves useful native phases
and noise. Pure gadget rotation, public norm tuning and zero-noise source
specialization do not close it. The plaintext-game and format-cycle routes
also still need their missing full construction/reduction. No new circular,
KDM, binary-source or leakage assumption, changed encryption key, or acyclic
key schedule has been introduced. The goal remains active and unachieved.

Verification: both Lean roots build successfully (9,247 jobs), and the
expanded GSWSelfKeyAudit checks 915 declarations using only propext,
Classical.choice and Quot.sound. The final module/build/audit logs contain no
Lean errors, warnings or sorryAx. All 471 selected authoritative Lean/config
files byte-match the validated container mirror. The executable completes;
its counts, private norm bounds, source identities and noisy half-turn lower
bounds are verified. Python syntax validation and git diff --check pass.
All 40 reference entries agree across catalog, bibliography, index, sizes and
SHA256 checksums; every new PDF page was parsed. No sub-agents were used.


Rethink 2026-10-10: public pair-supported actual-residual corrections
===================================================================

The preceding rotated-source trial failed because its canonical correction
used the noisy inverse proxy z=s+eta without repairing its actual phase. This
turn tries a different correction chosen from that public proxy. It also
checks whether successful public correction itself exposes a small key list.
The requested same-secret ordinary-LWE-only security theorem remains missing.

Construction tried
------------------
For distinct coordinates i,j and gadget weight w, the native quadratic target
is -w*s_i*s_j. Choose public coefficients (C,h_i,h_j), put C in the control
body and subtract h from its independently drawn raw mask. Its extra phase is

  delta(a,b) = C + h_i*a + h_j*b + w*a*b.

The four possible excluded assignments have three-corner-exact coefficients:

  excluded 00: (w,-w,-w); 01: (0,0,-w);
  excluded 10: (0,-w,0); 11: (0,0,0).

The residual is zero on the other three assignments and has magnitude |w|
on the excluded one. The body column and binary diagonal column retain their
exact affine corrections. GSWPairLocalCompiler.three_corner_residual
formalizes this construction.
No inverse error or secret is an input to the public coefficient algorithm.

For the finite attempt, z=H^{-T}*y_H is public and the excluded assignment is
(1-(z_i mod 2),1-(z_j mod 2)). Since z=s+eta modulo the even modulus, that
assignment equals the actual secret pair exactly when BOTH eta_i and eta_j
are odd. Consequently ALL pair corrections are exact iff the parity vector
of eta has Hamming weight at most one. This condition is checked after drawing
the source, never used to resample it. For this specific correction the
compatible key list always has exactly n+1 elements: the parity of z and its
n single-coordinate flips. The generic enumerator below does not rely on
this special form or on the inverse-proxy construction.

General public pair-supported barrier
-------------------------------------
Fix any public transcript, compiler coins and coefficients C,h_i,h_j. They
may depend nonlinearly on every source mask/body. Four-corner cancellation
still gives

  delta(1,1)-delta(1,0)-delta(0,1)+delta(0,0)=w.

The modular centered triangle inequality implies that at least one corner
has magnitude above B whenever 4B < |center(w)|. Let F be all binary keys
whose corrections have magnitude at most B for every coordinate pair.
F therefore cannot shatter any two-coordinate set. Sauer--Shelah with VC
dimension at most one gives |F| <= n+1. This argument covers arbitrary
pair-supported coefficients, not just a particular preimage certificate,
small coefficient norm, proxy, or three-corner algorithm.

The six checked declarations in GSWPairLocalCompiler are:
three_corner_residual, four_corner_identity, exists_bad_pair,
not_shatters_pair, card_le_of_no_shattered_pair and candidate_key_bound.
Binary keys are represented by their support finsets. The candidate bound
uses Mathlib's checked Sauer--Shelah theorem. The theorem is pointwise and
does not itself formalize an LWE game, asymptotic runtime or source reduction.

For native controls with total centered error at most N and raw fresh errors
at most E, compiler residuals have magnitude at most B=N+E. Thus applying the
barrier to the top weight w=q/2 requires N+E<q/8. One must check this condition;
an isolated correctly decoded output is not the same as that control bound.
The proof does not rule out a different evaluation invariant that tolerates
larger or specially correlated control errors.

Polynomial extraction and the finite source game
------------------------------------------------
For each pair, publicly calculate which assignments violate the residual
bound. Each excluded assignment (a,b) becomes the 2SAT clause
(s_i != a OR s_j != b). This describes F exactly. Use strongly connected
components to test feasibility after each candidate-prefix extension.
There are at most n+1 feasible prefixes at every depth, since each extends
a full candidate; at most 2n(n+1) feasibility calls suffice. Each graph has
2n vertices and O(n^2) edges, giving a straightforward O(n^4) enumeration.
This runtime argument is mathematical; the Python implementation is checked
against exhaustive enumeration on all complete pair systems for n<=3 and
128 fixed systems for n=4, totaling 198 systems including n=0,1.

Hold out one independent source sample (a_v,y_v), excluded from the compiler
inputs. Accept iff a listed candidate t obeys

  |center(y_v-t dot a_v)| <= E_v.

For real source samples, honest acceptance is at least 1-beta, where beta
includes compiler residual failures and verification-error tail failures.
For an ideal source, conditional on every compiler input and coin, y_v is
independent uniform. Each listed key accepts on at most 2E_v+1 body values
(assuming 2E_v+1<=q). Ideal acceptance is at most

  (n+1)*(2E_v+1)/q.

The decision advantage is therefore at least
1-beta-(n+1)*(2E_v+1)/q. An efficient public builder meeting the useful residual
and negligible-failure conditions would contradict the MATCHING binary-source
hardness. To conclude from ordinary LWE alone, instantiate the already proved
weak-secret reduction with the compiler's full source width, dimension,
modulus/noise and extra held-out sample, and retain all reduction losses.
No new independent binary-LWE assumption is adopted. This is a scoped
mathematical reduction argument, not a completed Lean computational theorem.
It says nothing about private coefficients without the public computation
interface, or corrections whose affine support includes other coordinates.

Finite evidence
---------------
Run PYTHONPATH=/tmp/fhe-preimage-packages python3
scripts/try-gsw-pair-local-compiler.py --output
docs/GSWPairLocalCompilerExploration.json.

Keys are uniform binary draws. A fixed pool of 64 source matrices supplies
the first invertible H; every source error is drawn once and no failed source
or key is discarded. All 32 selected source errors are nonzero. Small trials
use n=2,3,4, levels=16 for n=2 and 24 otherwise, E=1,64, four fixed seeds per
geometry. Eight n=16,32 trials use levels=16, E=1,64, two seeds per geometry;
these perform source/candidate extraction only, not refresh.

The executable checks 6,912 complete native-column phase identities and
144 refresh calls. Fourteen of the 24 small source cases have exact compiler
residuals; ALL their 84 refresh calls decode the selected bit correctly and
meet the full output decoding margin. Of those calls, 75 meet the sufficient
input-rounding margin and 62 meet the conservative next-NAND rounding margin.
These successful corrections repair the previous nonzero-error toy failure.
But their public candidate lists, followed by the held-out sample, recover
the actual key in ALL fourteen cases. They do not supply secure FHE.

The other ten small cases give 31/60 selected successes, one full output
margin and one next-NAND margin. Across all 144 calls the totals are 115
selected successes, 85 full output margins and 63 next-NAND margins. Isolated
successes in the failed cases do not restore the all-control invariant.
The eight larger trials have inverse-error parity weights 7..16; the true
key is absent from every public candidate list. Their lists contain 17 or
33 keys respectively. No security inference is based just on those finite
failure frequencies. No claim that eta is asymptotically uniform is needed
for the general source-distinguisher argument.

Remaining possible ways, with the next decisive obligation
----------------------------------------------------------
1. Dense public actual-phase corrections. Let each quadratic target use
   affine coefficients on additional secret coordinates. The new pair-only
   Sauer--Shelah proof does not apply. A useful construction must give an
   efficient sampler and a joint high-probability bound on every actual
   residual for the same source secret, including all gadget levels and
   repeated-refresh margins. Optimize the actual residual law rather than
   a certificate norm. Exact affine closure on the entire binary key space
   was already excluded by GSWFeatureClosure; approximate, transcript-specific
   dense rules still need a construction or a suitably scoped new barrier.
   Writing another theorem with an assumed residual bound does not resolve it.

2. Private trapdoor correction with a full-view source reduction. The private
   MP12-style backend already has promising finite correctness. What is
   missing is an efficient simulator for the JOINT public key and all stored
   controls at an ordinary-LWE challenge, including the private-witness
   correlations. Matrix marginal uniformity is insufficient. The rotated
   simulator solved body computation but lost the native error invariant.
   A new simulator must produce a different conditional error law, or prove
   its published marginal equals a useful simulatable law. Reusing the same
   noisy inverse projection or hiding its error-dependent basis leaves the
   already recorded gap. Flooding to conventional self-key GSW merely
   transfers the unresolved security obligation to route 3.

3. Direct complete-game reduction for native self-key GSW. Keep the native
   format and its existing correctness proofs. The missing step is an
   ordinary-LWE challenge embedding, or a computational replacement lemma,
   for ALL key-dependent controls jointly with the public key and adaptive
   evaluated ciphertexts. Exact stored-view transports and bit-flip symmetry
   are available. A conditional circular/KDM replacement bound is not the
   embedding. This route avoids a format conversion but is the central
   unresolved security problem, not an already justified hybrid step.

4. Change the same-secret ciphertext format and prove a closed refresh cycle.
   Affine-KDM/randomized-encoding/commitment-coin formats remain candidates
   only if their native decoder/evaluator is supplied or a compatible
   conversion is proved. Encrypted opening coins can track leveled operations,
   but refreshed metadata's own encryption coins create another dependency.
   The next decisive object is a fixed-size joint sampler and invariant that
   closes this metadata cycle under one unchanged encryption secret, together
   with its ordinary-LWE reduction. PS16's leveled commitment machinery alone
   does not do so. A growing metadata tower or fresh-key schedule is outside
   the objective.

The first two are remaining construction targets; the third is a direct
security-reduction target; the fourth requires a complete new format. None
currently meets all requested conditions. Pair-supported public repair and
short exact public preimage tuning are no longer promising completion targets
under their matching source/noise conditions. This is not a global
impossibility claim, and no new assumption or changed-key schedule is adopted.

Verification: both Lean roots build successfully (9,248 jobs). The expanded
GSWSelfKeyAudit checks 921 declarations using only propext,
Classical.choice and Quot.sound. Module/build/audit logs have no Lean errors,
warnings or sorryAx. All 472 authoritative Lean/config files byte-match the
validated mirror. Python syntax validation and git diff --check pass. The
32 source attempts, 198 solver cross-checks, native phase identities,
candidate-count bounds and grouped refresh/recovery totals were verified.
No additional paper was needed and no sub-agents were used.


Continuation 2026-10-10: correlated public midpoint errors
=========================================================

The previous turn made progress: a public two-coordinate correction can have
excellent toy refresh correctness while allowing a polynomial candidate-key
attack. This turn examines a different remaining possibility: tolerate large
but correlated errors rather than assume every control has tiny error.
It does not supply the requested ordinary-LWE-only same-secret FHE theorem.

Why test midpoint correlation before inventing a dense rule
---------------------------------------------------------
A public constant affine correction, even with coefficients on every secret
coordinate, cannot approximate a quadratic target uniformly on all keys with
residual B<|center(w)|/4. Freeze every other coordinate and apply the existing
four-corner identity. At weights w=4v the midpoint attains that lower bound:

  C=v, h_i=h_j=-2v,
  delta(a,b)=v*(1-2a)*(1-2b).

Here native target phase is -w*a*b, and the published mask is rawMask-h.
At the two lowest binary levels use C=floor(w/4), h_i=h_j=-floor(w/2), so
all coefficients are actual integers modulo the power-of-two modulus.
The body column keeps its exact linear correction. Two variants either keep
the exact binary diagonal correction or use the midpoint on the diagonal too.
In the latter, the high-level error matrix across control i and target j is
an outer product of the signed key bits. Errors are correlated, rather than
independent errors with magnitude q/8.

The coefficient rule contains no secret/source body. It is within the public
transcript-compiler source interface, whose existing security transport is a
triangular bijection. Thus the issue is actual native correctness, not a new
unproved circular-security premise. Security transport alone is insufficient
when the generated controls cannot implement the intended evaluator.
Transcript-dependent dense corrections are more general than this public
constant rule; the universal four-corner argument does not exclude them.

Formal native evaluator counterexample
--------------------------------------
GSWCorrelatedMidpoint.midpoint_residual checks the signed-product identity.
Five additional declarations use the actual GSWGadget.digitMatrix, multiply,
nand and decrypt with q=16, binary base and four levels, under the unchanged
zero binary secret of dimension two. No evaluation axiom is introduced.
The proofs use kernel-checked decide and norm_num, not native_decide.

The two controls are explicit realizations of the midpoint sampler with zero
fresh error: first index 0 has stored mask zero, and second index 1 has stored
mask 12 in both rows at every column. Any prescribed stored mask is attainable
by taking rawMask=storedMask+h. At the zero secret the raw LWE body is zero,
and the public constant correction supplies the body. These are pointwise
sampler realizations, not an assertion that zero-noise LWE is secure.

controls_decode_zero proves both controls decrypt to false. The theorem
controls_noise_at_most_eighth proves their native noise is at most 2=q/8
in EVERY column. product_body_phase proves that actual native multiplication
has body-top phase 6. Therefore product_decodes_wrong returns true when
false AND false should be false. nand_decodes_wrong returns false when NAND
should be true (its phase is 8-6=2). This counterexample disproves the proposed
universal multiplication invariant; by itself it says nothing about an
asymptotic failure probability or all conceivable correlated error laws.

Finite refresh trial
--------------------
Run PYTHONPATH=/tmp/fhe-preimage-packages python3
scripts/try-gsw-correlated-midpoint.py --output
docs/GSWCorrelatedMidpointExploration.json.

Seven fixed geometries (n,levels,E)=(2,16,0),(2,16,1),(2,16,64),
(3,24,1),(3,24,64),(4,24,1),(4,24,64), four fixed seeds each and the two
variants give 56 cases. All binary keys are uniform draws. All public masks
and errors are drawn once, with no correctness-conditioned resampling.
The same controls and secret are used for three refresh cycles on two
branches, including NAND continuations. All 14,592 complete native-column
phase identities and signed-product residual identities pass.

Across 336 refresh calls, exactly 168 selected bits decode correctly,
34 full output decoding margins pass and 18 sufficient next-NAND margins
pass. The exact-diagonal variant gives 11/24 selected successes at zero
source noise and 75/144 at positive radius; its output margins are 4/24
and 17/144, and next-NAND margins 1/24 and 11/144. The full signed-product
variant gives 13/24 zero-noise and 69/144 positive-radius selected successes;
its output margins are 3/24 and 10/144, and next-NAND margins 0/24 and 6/144.
The common maximum compiler residual is q/8 in every case. These results do
not establish a usable correlated-error refresh invariant.

Actual product-noise law and exact finite distribution checks
-------------------------------------------------------------
The second executable tests more than isolated decoded outputs. Run
PYTHONPATH=/tmp/fhe-preimage-packages python3
scripts/check-gsw-midpoint-product-law.py --output
docs/GSWMidpointProductLawExploration.json.

Let b be ANY fixed binary secret, first control index i and second index j.
For full midpoint controls, write t_k=1-2b_k. Consider their native product
at the top body-gadget column. Let U_k be the actual stored mask entry of the
second control in row k at that column. With zero fresh noise, its product
noise is exactly

  sum_k [t_i*t_k*floor(U_k/4)
         + b_i*b_k*(U_k mod 2)
         - (b_i XOR b_k)*(floor(U_k/2) mod 2)] mod q.

This follows from the existing GSW noise_multiply identity, the exact body
correction, and binary digit reconstruction. The lower two levels contribute
the last two terms; levels >=2 reconstruct floor(U_k/4). The Python check
exhausts q=16, every two-bit key, all four control-index pairs and all 256
selected mask-column pairs: 4,096 actual matrix product/phase identities.
It uses the public coefficient rule, not a secret-dependent correction.
For the exact-diagonal variant omit k=i from the compiler residual sum.

The honest raw masks are uniform, and their public constant shifts leave
U_k independently uniform modulo q. Conditional on their low two bits,
floor(U_k/4) are independent uniform on 0..q/4-1. Every signed sum has the
same law as a positive quarter-range convolution translated by a constant:
-U is distributed as U-(q/4-1) for a uniform quarter-range U. The lower-level
terms and claimed plaintext phase merely change that translation.

There are n quarter-range terms in the full variant and n-1 in the exact
binary-diagonal variant. Their modular convolution spreads the residual even
though the original control errors have rank-one structure. For any fixed
key and low-bit realization, correctness is bounded by the largest mass in
any circular nearest-codeword decoding window. Both bit decoders have at
most q/2+1 accepted phase values (ties decode to zero).

Bounded fresh noise does not require a new independence argument: native
noise_multiply adds e_first dot digitMatrix(second)+b_i*e_second. If every
fresh error has centered magnitude at most E and L=log2(q), the perturbation
has centered magnitude at most R=((n+1)*L+1)*E because each actual digit is
zero or one. A correct perturbed phase must have its baseline phase inside
the widened circular window of length min(q,q/2+1+2R). Thus the largest
convolution-window mass is an upper bound on product correctness, conditional
on the fresh-error bound. For unbounded errors, add their tail probability.
This is a scoped MATHEMATICAL distribution argument, not a completed Lean
probability or computational-security theorem.

The executable uses integer counts, sliding circular convolutions and exact
fractions, with no floating-point FFT approximations. Fifteen zero-error
cases cover q=64,256,1024 and 2,4,8,16,32 terms. Two additional full-variant
cases use q=65,536, E=1 and n=16,32. Their maximum widened-window masses are
0.6269256860988652 and 0.5382372649025561 respectively. These are upper bounds
under the stated product-law hypotheses for every fixed secret, not observed
success frequencies. As usual, those toy source parameters do not imply LWE
hardness. The calculation concerns this midpoint family alone.

Literature revalidation
-----------------------
A fresh primary-source check of AGHV25 again confirms that its
CPA-to-leveled-funcCPA result uses independent level keys, while its same-key
funcCPA-to-circular implication is a barrier rather than an ordinary-LWE
instantiation of the premise:
https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/s00145-024-09526-1

A new search hit, ECCC TR26-098, concerns formalizing leveled GSW reasoning
inside bounded arithmetic for a SNARG result with additional assumptions.
Its stated contribution does not supply same-secret bootstrapping security.
It was not imported as a security premise or added to the references, since
it is not needed for the algebra or counterexample above:
https://eccc.weizmann.ac.il/report/2026/098/

No qualifying complete ordinary-LWE same-secret construction was obtained
from this search. This is a report on the examined sources, not a theorem
that no such result can exist.

Completion audit and changed next action
---------------------------------------
The public midpoint sampler makes the source simulation straightforward, but
its signed-product errors do not support native GSW multiplication/refresh.
The counterexample and product law explain why tuning the maximum error to
q/8 is insufficient. This route has not yielded secure correct FHE.

A dense transcript-dependent sampler remains an unconstructed candidate,
with all actual residuals and full joint source security still needing proof.
A private full-marginal source simulator remains a different candidate; the
promising private correctness trials do not solve its joint-security gap.
Direct native full-game challenge embedding and closed same-secret metadata
formats also remain unproved. The next work must supply a new joint law or
challenge embedding, not merely assume a residual bound or increase q.
No new circular/KDM/binary-LWE/leakage assumption or acyclic key schedule
has been adopted. The goal is active and unachieved.

Completion and blocked-status audit, 2026-10-10
==============================================

The previous goal turn was progress: it added a checked native multiplication
counterexample and finite evidence that changes the next action. This audit
reopened the current sources and verified that no remaining concrete algorithm
or theorem closes the actual objective. It does not mark the goal complete.

Requirements and authoritative evidence
--------------------------------------
* Same-secret executable refresh and circuit evaluation: GSWAccumulator,
  GSWBootstrap, GSWBootstrapParameters and GSWCircuit contain public algorithms
  and noise/decryption theorems using one unchanged binary secret. Their
  correctness theorems require useful control/input noise bounds. This proves
  conditional native correctness, not existence of a secure control sampler.

* Complete joint security from ordinary LWE alone: GSWMasking's reduction
  still retains keyReplacementAdvantage for the public key plus controls.
  GSWStoredSecurityView.keyReplacementAdvantage_normalized transports this
  advantage exactly without bounding it. GSWMixedStoredView likewise provides
  exact distributions/symmetries, not the missing computational embedding.
  Ordinary-LWE semantic security of fresh public encryption does not itself
  settle security with all same-key bootstrapping controls published.

* A concrete public compiler with both security and correctness:
  GSWTranscriptCompiler.stored_game_abs_advantage_le gives the ordinary-source
  security reduction for its interface. Its discrepancy/control_noise and
  bootstrap theorems retain the actual residual/noise requirement. No useful
  dense transcript-dependent sampler meeting that requirement has been
  supplied. The pair-supported sampler exposes a small key list; the canonical
  rotated sampler fails control margins; the midpoint sampler fails native
  multiplication and the tested refresh cycles. Passing the public compiler's
  conditional security theorem cannot complete this requirement.

* A concrete private compiler with both security and correctness:
  GSWPrivateTrapdoor.certificate and projected_body establish algebraic
  identities, and the finite private-trapdoor experiments support correctness
  at toy parameters. GSWPrivateTrapdoor.programmed_source_body explicitly
  retains the unknown G-secret and base-error terms when extending an LWE
  challenge. There is no implementation or theorem for a simulator of the
  complete published public-key/control view at that challenge. Near-uniformity
  of the matrix marginal is insufficient to substitute for this joint law.

* Recursive-feature or other format closure:
  RecursiveFeatureControlPolynomial.family_oneTime_abs_signedAdvantage_le
  still contains familyControlReplacementAdvantage. Its genuinely cubic
  complete-view hop is not bounded by ordinary LWE. Existing KDM/star conversion
  results do not supply the unknown-key controls or a closed secure refresh
  format. The metadata-cycle direction has no fixed-size reusable joint
  sampler/invariant. These are remaining mathematical questions, not ready
  implementations or proved primitive transformations.

* Efficiency, parameters and negligible loss for ONE compatible construction:
  sampled-law reductions, sampler approximations and parameter bounds exist
  for parts of the routes above, but no single actual construction has all
  required security and repeatable-correctness evidence. Consequently the
  requested final theorem for every admissible efficient adversary and
  polynomial evaluation cannot be assembled from the current components.
  No independent circular/KDM/binary-LWE/leakage assumption or changed-key
  scheduling may be inserted to hide this missing step.

* Lean verification: the existing 9,249-job build and 927-declaration axiom
  audit pass, using only propext, Classical.choice and Quot.sound. This audit
  rechecked all 473 authoritative Lean/config files against the validated
  mirror and rechecked the axiom output. These checks validate the statements
  actually proved; they do not establish a stronger security theorem absent
  from the statements. No new proof edits or redundant rebuild were warranted.

Blocked-status decision
-----------------------
The genuine blocker is the absence of a compatible efficient construction
and complete joint ordinary-LWE reduction, not a tooling/build failure or
pending computation. The same condition persisted through the three preceding
rotated-source, pair-correction and correlated-midpoint goal turns. Those
turns did make progress by excluding specific attempted repairs. At the end
of this audit, however, no further concrete sampler or valid challenge
embedding is available to implement and verify from the reviewed arguments.
Listing dense corrections/private simulation again, assuming their missing
bounds, or adding another conditional wrapper would not complete the goal.

The agent is now at an operational mathematical impasse. Resuming meaningful
constructive proof work requires a new concrete construction or reduction
argument, supplied by further research or an external proof/reference. This
is not a theorem that all such constructions are impossible or that the
objective can never be achieved. The full objective and all restrictions
remain intact. update_goal returned status BLOCKED on 2026-10-10; it did not
