Requirements checked against current authoritative sources
---------------------------------------------------------
1. Actual encryption and full public view: representation/experiment theorems
   are present. GSWMasking.operationalView retains the public key and controls;
   its operationalGame identity uses the real encryption algorithm. The
   recursive familyObserver likewise passes the entire stored public view
   to the IND-CPA adversary. These interfaces are AVAILABLE. Their existence
   does not bound the correlated-view security term.

2. Correct repeated evaluation under one unchanged key: AVAILABLE for the
   recursive-feature family. RecursiveFeatureFHECorrectness.familyCorrectExp_eq_scheme
   identifies the scheme API, and eventually_family_correctExp_probability_one
   proves correctness for every finite shared NAND program at sufficiently
   large parameters. The same generated controls and underlying secret are
   reused. This result is correctness only; its source explicitly records
   the missing joint security and strict efficiency obligations.

3. Security of the native GSW publication: INCOMPLETE.
   GSWMasking.operational_game_abs_advantage_le still has
   keyReplacementAdvantage for the actual public-key/control generator.
   The masking loss is separate. No ordinary-LWE bound for that complete
   correlated replacement has been found in the current worktree.

4. Security of the already correct recursive-feature family: INCOMPLETE.
   RecursiveFeatureControlPolynomial.family_oneTime_abs_signedAdvantage_le
   still has familyControlReplacementAdvantage. Its other terms are the
   quadratic ordinary-LWE reduction loss, implementation loss and masking
   loss. Searching the authoritative Lean sources finds the cubic term's
   definition and its use in this bound, without a theorem making it
   negligible or bounding it by ordinary-LWE advantages. This is the
   decisive missing computational proof for this construction.

5. Exact reduction target: the actual plaintext-game observer is sufficient;
   a stronger generic full-KDM theorem is not necessary. What is MISSING is
   a concrete efficient challenge embedding/reduction for that observer,
   with a finite advantage bound in terms of ordinary-LWE advantages and
   negligible statistical terms. It must generate every other correlated
   cubic column as well. Renaming the term or assuming it negligible would
   introduce the unresolved security premise.

6. One public compiler with both ordinary-LWE security and useful reusable
   correctness: INCOMPLETE. GSWTranscriptCompiler.stored_game_abs_advantage_le
   supplies an ordinary-source loss for a specified compiler, but
   noiseBound_bootstrap explicitly requires herror for the actual source
   error plus discrepancy. There is no concrete efficient compiler meeting
   that premise and the required reachable-state margins with a negligible
   complete loss. Security of one sampler cannot be combined with correctness
   of a different sampler to satisfy the goal.

7. The two newly tested direct simulator templates: neither closes item 4.
   Known-bit conditioning exactly reduces a covered cubic to a quadratic,
   but full-table coverage leaves at most two unknown key bits. The fixed
   independent guessing probability is at most 4/2^n, and the old entropy
   estimate cannot become negligible after that conditioning. The affine
   source theorem gives at least 1/16 phase failure under its uniform-mask
   and independence premises, including fixed key transport. Neither is a
   public security attack or an impossibility theorem for general FHE.

8. Nonlinear simulation, public mask rejection and mask-dependent key
   coupling: still OPEN, with no complete new candidate to instantiate.
   The specific public-rejection diagnostic fixes the zero-error cubic
   identity but fails on 1/4 of keys for odd source error. It has no fresh
   output distribution theorem. Requiring zero/even source errors is not
   a proved ordinary-LWE replacement. The general affine filter cannot be
   extended to arbitrary nonlinear maps by assertion; those routes remain
   mathematically unexcluded rather than implemented or verified.

9. Private key-correlated sampling: still OPEN. GSWPrivateTrapdoor proves
   the certificate and private projected-body identity, and its
   programmed_source_body explicitly retains the source gadget-secret term
   and transformed error. A matrix marginal, an undisclosed short witness,
   or private independent coins do not supply a simulator for the complete
   public law. No such simulator satisfying correctness and the original
   source assumption is available in the current candidates.

10. A specialized certified helper: still OPEN. The previously checked WZ17
    target-entropy guarantee does not implement the proposed publicly
    accepted-input helper. Fixed payloads also fail reusable output privacy.
    No helper with the required public input language, hidden output
    randomness and same-secret ordinary-LWE proof has been constructed.

11. Efficiency, compactness and asymptotic security packaging: INCOMPLETE
    for the combined construction. Polynomial parameter/sample-count and
    modulus-bit-length results exist, but they do not prove that all actual
    algorithms and reductions belong to the required strict polynomial-time
    classes. There is no final all-parameter security theorem for one
    construction satisfying all preceding requirements. A new assumption
    or a changed-key schedule is not authorized as a replacement.

2026-10-17 rethink: error-absorbing keys and a public-only dual-mode target
========================================================================
This user-requested rethink tests a concrete escape from the affine-source
premises. The output key now depends on source masks and errors. It repairs
the previously amplified odd error for a restricted family of top-weight
cubic controls. The subsequent privacy and uniform-source checks explain
why that repair alone is not a security reduction.

An actual mask/error-dependent key coupling
-----------------------------------------
Take n=m+2 binary source coordinates, q=2^ell, w=q/2, uniform source masks
A in (Z/qZ)^(n by m), and source outputs y=s^T*A+e. Let bars denote parity.
Write R=bar(A)^T=(H | B), with H having two columns and B invertible over F2.
Rejecting masks until B is invertible has constant expected cost; it is a
public rejection event, not a claim that the original uniform-mask law is
unchanged. Define privately

  delta = (0,0,B^-1*bar(e)),
  u = s XOR delta.

Then R*u=bar(y). The two head bits are unchanged, and the tail absorbs the
source-error parities. For every fixed accepted A and e, the map s -> u is
a bijection on binary keys, so the output key is marginally uniform. Its
coupling to the input source key depends on A and e; it is outside the
fixed-transport hypotheses of RecursiveFeatureAffineSource.

Publicly compute

  d = B^-1*(H*(1,1)^T - bar(y)).

The relation R*u=bar(y) gives d=u_tail+B^-1*H*((1,1)-u_head), hence

  u_0*u_1*d_k = u_0*u_1*u_(k+2)  in F2.

For each tail coordinate construct a recursive ciphertext D_k with only
matrix entry T[0,1]=w*d_k nonzero; vector and body are zero. Its actual
RecursiveQuadratic phase is -w*u_0*u_1*d_k, which equals the desired
w*u_0*u_1*u_(k+2), since -w=w modulo q. This works even with odd source
errors. It handles only top-weight triples sharing the two head indices,
not the complete original-bit control table or all gadget levels. Every
tested control in a draw uses the same fixed output key u.

What the public output reveals
------------------------------
If the raw source (A,y) is published, enumerating the two head bits and
solving with B^-1 lists exactly four possible output keys. This statement
requires raw-source disclosure and is not silently applied when the raw
source is omitted from the output.

Even without that disclosure, the BARE controls reveal d, since each
nonzero entry is either zero or w. The guess (1,1,d) equals the ENTIRE
output key exactly when u_0=u_1=1. Under its uniform marginal this happens
with probability 1/4. Thus the unmasked repair has a concrete key-disclosure
problem, not merely a large conservative noise estimate. This is not an
attack on arbitrary mask-dependent key generation or on the current native
recursive-family sampler.

Fresh masks repair this exposure but do not supply the desired game hop
---------------------------------------------------------------------
Add an independent fresh private encryption of zero under u to D_k. Its
phase becomes the desired cubic plus the normal fresh error f+u^T*e'. The
executable checks this exact native phase and its usual (n+1)*radius bound.

There is also a simple exact distribution calculation. Write the fresh
zero ciphertext as (T,c=u^T*T-a+e',b=u^T*a+f), and write D for its matrix
correction. Its phase relation is -u^T*D*u=message. Changing private masks to

  T_new=T+D,  a_new=a+u^T*D

preserves their uniform joint law, keeps c unchanged, and makes
u^T*a_new+message+f=b. Thus the sum has the genuine fresh private ciphertext
law for that cubic message, conditional on u. This is a mathematical
change-of-variables proof, not a new Lean distribution theorem. It concerns
this restricted control batch; no full public-key/refresh sampler is supplied.

If preserving the original source-key coupling, the latent head bits are
unknown to the reduction. A public-only simulator need not preserve that
coupling: it can choose head bits alpha,beta itself and compute

  u_tail=B^-1*(bar(y)-H*(alpha,beta)^T).

It now knows one valid latent key and can generate the fresh-zero masks.
However, in the REAL source, bar(y) is uniform conditional on accepted A,
because the original source tail is uniform and B invertible. In the
UNIFORM source, bar(y) is also uniform. Uniform chosen head bits therefore
give a uniform output key in BOTH source modes. After the private mask
change above, the hidden-source output is the same fresh cubic-control
distribution in both modes. Correctness was repaired, but the uniform mode
did not become the quadratic/zero comparison or a statistically hiding
challenge experiment.

Publishing the raw source reintroduces the four-key list. Omitting it and
freshly generating an independent native public key under the known latent
key would reproduce the real key/control law in both modes; it would not
create a distinguishing gap for the ordinary-LWE reduction. Publicly adding
zero encryptions instead requires a compatible target public-key sampler,
which this experiment does not construct. These distinctions prevent an
incorrect claim that latent-key regeneration already proves the cubic hop.

Revised remaining proof targets
------------------------------
1. Prioritize a mode-sensitive PUBLIC-GAME proof for the already correct
   recursive family. The real-source simulator must match its complete
   public key, control table and actual IND-CPA observer. The uniform-source
   simulator must instead have winning probability 1/2 plus negligible loss.
   It need not preserve a valid hidden decryption key in that mode. A concrete
   lossy mode with conditional challenge masking is one possible mechanism.
   The new diagnostic demonstrates why merely matching the real public
   distribution is insufficient: both source modes can match it identically.
   No such useful uniform endpoint is constructed here.

2. A private coupled sampler remains possible, but its first checks must
   include efficiently enumerable key lists, actual public disclosure, and the full
   real/uniform source experiment. Fixing the odd-error phase identity alone
   can leak the output key. Adding fresh private masks can restore the real
   ciphertext law without proving security. A new candidate must give the
   complete public sampler in both modes and the same reusable correctness
   invariant in its real mode. Nonlinear or mask-dependent processing remains
   allowed; the affine restriction is not promoted to a general obstruction.
   Avoiding the specific four-key list is a computational requirement here,
   not a claim that ordinary-LWE security requires large information-theoretic
   key entropy conditioned on the entire public view.

3. A specialized certified helper is still a different construction route,
   with the earlier accepted-input and hidden-output-randomness requirements.
   It remains lower priority until an appropriate ordinary-LWE public-helper
   primitive is identified. Generic compute-and-compare or static reusable
   encoding has not supplied that interface in the sources checked so far.

These are concrete proof interfaces to aim for, not complete constructions
or an exhaustive classification of every possible approach. No independent
circular/KDM/leakage assumption or changing encryption-key schedule is added.
The actual familyControlReplacementAdvantage bound remains unproved.

Related primary source
----------------------
PVW08, A Framework for Efficient and Composable Oblivious Transfer, is saved
in refs/dual-mode/PVW08.pdf. The retrieved 28-page author-hosted draft is
dated September 20, 2007, corresponding to CRYPTO 2008. Sections 7.2-7.3
give LWE pseudorandom-public-key and message-lossy-key techniques; the lattice
dual-mode variant has a relaxed predetermined polynomial call bound per CRS.
This is technique input, not a theorem about the GSW evaluation-key view.
  https://www.mit.edu/~vinodv/papers/OT.pdf
  https://eprint.iacr.org/2007/348

Our application still needs mode hiding with the entire key-dependent
control table retained. A bound on setup calls must be matched to actual
setup usage; repeated deterministic refresh is not automatically an extra
OT call. No OT protocol, CRS trust requirement, or claimed same-key FHE
result is imported merely by citing this paper.

An additional reason to deprioritize this particular repair is that its
public-only implementation uses only (A,bar(y)) and independent coins.
Conditional on accepted A, that projection has EXACTLY the same law for
real and uniform source outputs: the full-rank binary tail already makes
bar(y) uniform. Every public postprocessing of this projection therefore
has zero source distinguishing advantage, regardless of the observer's
runtime. This is a data-processing observation about the tested source law,
not a claim that general LWE parities or full-residue transcripts have that
property. A next mode-sensitive candidate must retain correlations lost by
this projection. Using higher residue information alone is not a proof;
the real endpoint and hiding uniform endpoint still need to be established.

Artifacts and verification scope
--------------------------------
scripts/try-gsw-error-absorbing-key.py and
docs/GSWErrorAbsorbingKeyExploration.json record 32 accepted source families,
2,752 enumerated source keys, 14,656 real cubic-phase checks, 14,656 real
fresh-zero-added phase checks, 14,656 uniform-source cubic-phase checks and
14,656 uniform-source fresh-zero-added checks. Every family includes an odd
source error. All bare-control key guesses succeed on exactly one quarter
of keys; every published raw source gives exactly four candidates. The
uniform-mode nonzero cubic-message fraction is exactly 1/8. These are exact
finite arithmetic checks and the mathematical derivations above, not new
Lean theorems, IND-CPA advantage bounds, asymptotic hardness evidence or a
complete FHE construction.

Correlated controls in the uniform-table endpoint, 2026-10-17
===========================================================
The goal has resumed; get_goal reports ACTIVE. The preceding rethink counts
as PROGRESS: it implemented a mask/error-dependent latent-key coupling,
checked its privacy and both source modes, and saved PVW08. Its endpoint
failure changes the next action to a real public-game mode comparison. The
completed trial is terminal, not a verified wait.

This continuation proves a positive relaxation of the missing uniform
endpoint. Controls do not have to be independent of the public-key table,
and the entire uniform-source output does not have to be uniform or generated
by a bijection. The endpoint only requires uniformity of the FULL table
marginal, including all body and mask rows, together with fresh independent
encryption selectors. This is stronger than the previously available
GSWMasking.uniformView theorem, which resamples the table independently of
the old context.

The exact correlated-context calculation
---------------------------------------
Let V=(T,Z) be any finite joint public view. T is the public-key table and
Z can be the complete stored bootstrapping controls. Let H_T(R) be the
whole ciphertext hash core, with independent uniform selector tape R, and
let U be an independent uniform core. Then

  TV( (V,H_T(R)), (V,U) )
    = sum_(t,z) Pr[V=(t,z)] * TV(H_t(R),U)
    = sum_t Pr[T=t] * TV(H_t(R),U).

The second equality only sums out Z. It does NOT require T and Z to be
independent, and does not construct or assume an efficient sampler for Z
conditioned on T. The checked context_joint_tvDist_eq_marginal proves this
identity for arbitrary finite seed, context and output types.

If T has the uniform table marginal, the existing seeded leftover-hash
lemma supplies the usual bound, with K=(dimension+1)*levels:

  K * sqrt(Q^(dimension+1) / 2^publicSamples) / 2.

hashCore_context_masking retains the COMPLETE view in this bound. The
subsequent game bridge uses the actual stored-ciphertext public encryption
and adaptive message choice. Under the uniform core, the gadget translation
hides the selected message for every fixed table/context pair. The checked
game_abs_advantage_le_of_uniform_table_marginal therefore bounds the actual
IND-CPA winning probability minus 1/2 by the same statistical expression,
with no context-size multiplier or independence premise.

The context is finite, as are the actual stored control types. The adversary's
state is unrestricted in this proof. Contexts correlated with the FUTURE
encryption selector coins are not in this experiment: fresh selectors are
sampled independently after the public view. No extra security assumption
is introduced by the statistical result.

A broader ordinary-LWE simulator interface
-----------------------------------------
ordinary_source_simulator_game_abs_advantage_le takes an ordinary batch-LWE
problem with uniform ZMod secret and an explicit error sampler, plus any
randomized public function

  S : ordinary source transcript -> ProbComp (Table x Context).

S may be nonlinear and may discard information; its output need not be an
invertible transform of the source. Its controls may depend arbitrarily on
the entire source and public table. The sole uniform-endpoint condition is

  distribution(fst(S(uniform source))) = uniform full Table.

The theorem constructs the actual source distinguisher: given raw source,
run S, then run the complete GSWMasking IND-CPA continuation with fresh coins.
The resulting finite advantage bound is

  |Pr[win with S(real ordinary-LWE source)] - 1/2|
    <= Adv_ordinaryLWE(this explicit distinguisher)
       + K * sqrt(Q^(dimension+1) / 2^publicSamples) / 2.

The identity between real/uniform source games and the public-view games
is proved, not adopted as a new joint-view hardness premise. The separate
game_abs_advantage_le_joint_mode_gap also permits a direct game comparison
to ANY uniform-table marginal endpoint, including correlated controls.

What this changes for construction work
---------------------------------------
It is unnecessary to make the entire ideal control view uniform, or to
preserve its independence from the public table. For example, if a source
simulator keeps a public-key projection whose marginal is uniform on uniform
source input, arbitrary additional public processing of that source into
controls does not add a uniform-endpoint masking obligation. This observation
does not claim that those controls are correct or useful for refresh.

The current GSWTranscriptCompiler security theorem uses a bijective full-view
source transform. The new interface permits randomized, non-invertible,
body-dependent samplers outside that template. A real candidate still has
to match the correctly refreshing generator's public law (or define a new
correct generator), implement all original-bit control columns, and prove
the real-mode error invariant. It must also make the explicit reduction and
algorithms efficient and instantiate ordinary-LWE hardness/negligible losses
at one compatible parameter family. None of those construction obligations
is silently discharged by the endpoint theorem.

In particular, a private trapdoor sampler's nearly uniform MASK MATRIX
marginal is not enough: the full public table includes its LWE body rows.
Nor can ordinary-LWE pseudorandomness of that table alone be lifted to a
joint table/control mode-hiding theorem. The real-to-uniform-source full
view still has to be generated by a valid public simulator or handled by a
separate proved plaintext-game reduction. The existing correctly refreshing
recursive family still retains familyControlReplacementAdvantage.

The preceding error-absorbing sampler does not automatically meet the new
uniform-table endpoint: its repaired-key fresh public-key law is valid in
both source modes, rather than a uniform full table in the uniform mode.
The new result removes an unnecessary control-independence requirement;
it does not change that experiment's failed mode comparison into a proof.

Artifacts and proof scope
-------------------------
GSWCorrelatedMasking adds eight results: the exact context/marginal TV
identity, full-view hash masking, actual-game equality, ideal-game fairness,
the postprocessed TV bound, adaptive game masking under a uniform marginal,
the joint-mode game comparison, and the explicit ordinary-source reduction.
The root library and focused GSW audit import the new module. No encryption,
key-generation, refresh algorithm, noise law, key schedule or existing
correctness theorem is changed. The reference collection remains 45 papers.
This is the first continuation after the fresh resumption; the original
goal is ACTIVE and unfinished, with the positive uniform endpoint now proved.

2026-10-17 continuation: retaining or regenerating the public encryption key
=========================================================================
This is the second continuation after the fresh resumption. The goal remains
ACTIVE and unfinished. The positive correlated-context endpoint is already
checked. This continuation tests a concrete way of instantiating it, rather
than changing that theorem's hypotheses: combine the preceding error-absorbing
latent key with the actual recursive public encryption columns.

The two versions of the candidate
---------------------------------
The earlier parity-syndrome construction replaces the original binary source
key s by u=s XOR delta, where delta=(0,0,B^-1 e_mod_2). The invertible tail
matrix B and a forced odd source-error coordinate ensure delta is nonzero.
Thus u differs from s for every key in this diagnostic. The construction
repairs restricted top-weight cubic controls sharing two head bits, with
normal phase errors after fresh zero-encryption addition. A full construction
would also need public encryption to work under that SAME output key u.

Version A keeps fresh recursive zero columns generated under s and decrypts
the resulting public ciphertexts with u. Version B regenerates those columns
under u. Both versions use exactly the earlier error-absorbing key mapping;
there is no acyclic key schedule or sequence of refresh keys. This trial
checks initial encryption compatibility before attempting repeated refresh.

An exact wrong-key phase calculation
------------------------------------
A genuine fresh recursive zero column under s has

  T uniform, a uniform,
  c = s^T T - a + e',      b = <s,a> + f.

Under another binary key v its phase is

  b + <v,c> - v^T T v
    = f + <v,e'> + <s-v,a> + (s-v)^T T v.

For v != s, some coefficient s_i-v_i is +1 or -1, hence a unit even over
Z/(2^L)Z. After fixing T, errors, and the other coordinates of a, varying
a_i uniformly makes this phase EXACTLY uniform. Independent fresh columns
give independent such phases for every fixed v. If every error coordinate
is in [-E,E], the correct key has phase norm at most H=(n+1)E. For H<q/2,
the exact probability that a fixed wrong binary key satisfies that bound
on all p columns is

  pi^p,       pi = (2H+1)/q.

A union bound over the 2^n-1 wrong keys bounds the existence of ANY alternative
compatible binary key by (2^n-1) pi^p. This existence event also covers a key
chosen adaptively after seeing the entire table. It does not require the
chosen wrong key to be independent of that table. The independent fresh-column
law is the premise; no corresponding assertion is made for every correlated
or transformed table law. This calculation is a mathematical derivation and
finite diagnostic, not a new Lean theorem.

The actual selected public-encryption column is the subset sum of these
recursive columns, plus bit*q/2 in the body at the constant-feature top gadget
column. Under s its noise norm is at most pH. With pH<q/4, it decodes correctly.
The script computes this column component by component and applies the actual
recursive decryption formula. It does not test a complete GSW matrix or a
bootstrap execution; failure of this column already violates the intended
initial decryption check under u.

Why regeneration fails the particular uniform endpoint
-------------------------------------------------------
For a FULL uniform table of recursive columns (T,c,b), the body b itself is
independently uniform. Therefore the phase under each fixed binary v is
uniform on every column. The probability that there exists ANY binary v
whose zero-column phase norms are all at most H is bounded by

  2^n pi^p.

Call this event Good. An algorithm that always regenerates a narrow-noise
zero table under some binary key has Pr[Good]=1, regardless of how that key
was selected or whether it was kept private. Its full table marginal is
therefore at TV distance at least 1-2^n pi^p from the uniform table marginal.
With correctness failure eta, the same argument gives 1-eta-2^n pi^p.
The exponent here is the ORIGINAL binary-key dimension n, not the larger
n*n+n recursive feature dimension: the witness keys are actual featureSecret
vectors of binary keys.

Good may require exhaustive key search to decide. This support-event argument
does NOT contradict ordinary-LWE computational pseudorandomness of real public
keys, give an efficient attack on them, or show that a correct real endpoint
cannot be computationally indistinguishable from a uniform endpoint. It rules
out ALWAYS regenerating a valid narrow-noise table in BOTH source modes as a
way of obtaining the specific uniform-table marginal in GSWCorrelatedMasking.
Changing the joint table law in a different way remains outside this filter.

-------------------

docs/GSWRegeneratedPublicViewExploration.json. It imports the prior candidate's
parity inversion, key transport, fresh-zero sampler and decryption formula.
The deterministic trial uses n in {3,4,6,8}, q in {2^14,2^20}, bounded-error
radius in {1,3}, p in {8,24}, and two seeds: 64 families and 5,504 enumerated
source keys. These diagnostic errors are not an asymptotic ordinary-LWE
hardness instantiation. One control-source error is deliberately odd.

Results:
* All 88,064 original-key column bounds pass.
* Under u, 87,974 of those 88,064 columns violate the same bound, and none
  of the 5,504 retained tables passes all of its column bounds.
* All 88,064 regenerated-key column bounds pass under u.
* All 22,016 selected public-encryption columns pass the strict q/4 interval
  under their original keys, and all 22,016 regenerated-table columns pass
  under u. With the retained table and u, 10,954 of 22,016 fail that interval.
  The actual nearest-codeword decoder, including its zero-biased tie rule,
  also returns the wrong bit on exactly those 10,954 tested columns.
* Exhaustive enumeration of all binary keys for the first retained table in
  each family finds the original key as the unique compatible key in all
  64 cases. These are unbounded diagnostic enumerations.
* No sampled uniform table in the 64 families has a compatible binary key.
  In every family the exact rational union bound is below 2^-40. The formula,
  rather than these 64 samples, justifies the uniform-endpoint exclusion.
* Separately, 544 complete small-modulus inner-mask coordinate orbits and

  uniform phase multisets. These are finite checks, not a general proof.

2026-10-18 rethink: square-zero secrets, source lifting, and bit controls
======================================================================
The user requested a fresh rethink after the blocked completion audit. The
full ordinary-LWE-only, repeatably bootstrappable GSW objective is preserved.
get_goal still reports the last returned BLOCKED status; this entry records
new user-authorized construction work, not a completed security proof or a
new three-turn blocked audit. No resumed ACTIVE status is asserted here.

The new question is whether a different secret algebra can eliminate the
nonlinear self-key messages while preserving native GSW evaluation. Unlike
changing a binary key after generating its public table, this candidate
constructs the table and controls jointly from the outset. It gives a useful
explicit source lift, but fails the required Boolean-control interface.

An ordinary-LWE source lift with a square-zero encryption secret
----------------------------------------------------------------
Take even Q>=2, ciphertext modulus q=Q^2, ordinary source secret x in (Z/QZ)^n,
and output secret S=Q*x in (Z/qZ)^n. Every product S_i*S_j is zero modulo q.
For a source column (B,y), where y=<x,B>+e modulo Q, choose independent high
mask coordinates H uniform in Z/QZ and f uniform in a centered interval of
Q consecutive integers. For a PUBLIC known shift d, output

  A = (B-d modulo Q) + Q*H,       b = Q*y + f modulo q.

The real output has exact phase

  b - <S,A> = Q*e + f + Q*<x,d> modulo q.

The error term is bounded by Q*E+Q/2 when |e|<=E. For d=0 this gives fresh
zero columns under the unchanged output secret S. For a native GSW control
whose scalar plaintext target is S_i, every mask-block message
-S_i*S_j*g vanishes. Its body-block message is S_i*g. Choose d=g*e_i for the
body block and d=0 elsewhere. Thus ALL its normalized self-coordinate phase
targets are obtained from publicly shifted ordinary source masks. The
source simulator uses neither x nor e. Conditional on x, the stored masks
are uniform, and the phase/error law is that of native fresh columns under
S with error Q*e+f. Reparametrizing the original secret-dependent GSW mask
shift only changes the high uniform mask coordinates in this square-zero
case, so it introduces no new distribution premise.

In the uniform source branch, the map (B,y,H,f)->(A,b) is a BIJECTION for
each fixed public shift. Recover f as the centered representative of b mod Q;
then y=(b-f)/Q mod Q, B=(A mod Q)+d mod Q, and H=floor(A/Q). Consequently the
FULL columns, bodies included, are uniform. Applying this independently to
the whole source batch preserves that conclusion jointly for the table and
all self-coordinate controls. This avoids the earlier error-absorbing-key
candidate's failure to produce a uniform table in the uniform branch.

Mathematically this supplies the usual finite source reduction: compose the
public lift with the actual GSWMasking encryption-game observer, then use
the proved uniform-table masking endpoint. The source modulus here is Q,
whereas the ciphertext modulus is Q^2. The currently written
GSWCorrelatedMasking.ordinary_source_simulator_game_abs_advantage_le fixes
these moduli to be equal, so it is NOT directly instantiated by assertion.
The general joint-mode game bound applies, and the same source-game identity
would give Adv_LWE_Q plus K*sqrt(q^(n+1)/2^p)/2, with K=(n+1)*levels.
This cross-modulus derivation is mathematical, not a newly checked Lean
theorem or a completed asymptotic security instantiation. The source secret
can be uniform Z/QZ, so no independent binary-secret assumption is being
introduced by the lift. The finite diagnostic also tests binary x values;
those additional test inputs are not a binary-LWE hardness claim.

Why this still does not bootstrap
----------------------------------
The scalar targets S_i are NOT the bits of the secret-key encoding. Those
bits contain the bits of x_i. Standard GSW Boolean refresh consumes bit
controls; having the displayed phase identities for S_i does not supply them.
Nor does this claim a correct ring-message decryption interface for every
possible scalar S_i. The scheme's native nearest-codeword decoder is a bit
decoder, and the distinction must be maintained.

There is an exact arithmetic obstruction to a GENERIC plaintext-polynomial
rescaler taking Q*b to the bit b. For each fixed polynomial, all scaled
inputs are zero modulo Q, so the output modulo Q is its constant term.
It cannot output both 0 and 1 on inputs 0 and Q. Likewise a fixed polynomial
on the S_i has a key-independent constant residue and cannot extract their
varying bits. This is not a theorem excluding a converter specialized to
key-correlated auxiliary public data; that can vary the selected polynomial
with the public view. Restricting x to binary values does not fix the generic
plaintext-polynomial obstruction.

Encoding Boolean messages themselves as Q*b also loses multiplication. On
inputs z0,z1 in {0,Q}, all products of two input values vanish modulo q, so
EVERY ring polynomial restricts to c+a*z0+b*z1. Its mixed second difference
on the four Boolean corners is zero, while Q*(bit0 AND bit1) has mixed
second difference Q != 0. Thus even encoded AND cannot be implemented by
these ring-polynomial operations. Native encoded NAND similarly returns Q
on all corners when formed as Q-z0*z1. These exclusions concern this
plaintext encoding and polynomial operations, not every possible public
nonlinear rescaling, modulus-switching or auxiliary-dependent conversion.

The fine error is essential to the proposed lift. If f is omitted, every
public zero-column body is divisible by Q. The body-feature gadget column
of weight one in public encryption then reveals the message bit directly
through its body coordinate modulo Q. The independent uniform fine part
removes that deterministic residue disclosure and makes the full uniform
source map bijective. Omitting it is not a valid simplification.

A concrete public bit-rescaling attempt
----------------------------------------
For binary x, start with the control's level-zero BODY-feature column, using
d=e_i above. Publicly recover its coarse source body y from b and the centered
fine residue. At top weight g=q/2, form the candidate column

  A'=(g/Q)*(A mod Q),       b'=g*y modulo q.

It uses only the retained public column and Q, and keeps the SAME secret S.
Its phase is exactly g*(x_i+e), not g*x_i plus a small residual. Thus every
odd source error flips its actual nearest-codeword decoding; every even
source error passes this selected-column test. The failure is present after
the fine part has been removed exactly, so it cannot be repaired by choosing
a different rounding convention for that part. This is only a BODY-feature
attempt, not a complete compiler for the missing mask-block quadratic phases.
It already fails the easier body check with odd source noise. Requiring an
all-even or noiseless source would need a separate ordinary-LWE derivation.

Finite evidence and artifacts
------------------------------
scripts/check-gsw-nilpotent-secret.py writes
docs/GSWNilpotentSecretExploration.json. The deterministic executable checks:
* 48 phase families at Q=4,8,16,64, n=1,2,3, source radius 0 or 1, two seeds,
  and 416 binary/nonbinary key draws: all 3,328 public-zero phases, 24,480
  self-coordinate control phases, and 2,336 square-zero products pass.
* 15 exact single-column uniform-source bijections at Q=2,4,8,16 and public
  shifts 0,1,Q/2,Q (deduplicated): 279,600 distinct outputs with checked
  inverses, covering every possible full output column in each instance.
* All 69,904 restrictions c+a*z0+b*z1 for the small rings: none implements
  encoded AND on all four corners; the best agreement is three corners.
  All 4,680 one-input restrictions fail exact normalized bit extraction.
* All 2,048 public-encryption residue checks without fine error disclose
  the selected bit exactly, for all subsets of an eight-column body table.
* All 952 public body-rescale phase checks pass the displayed error identity;
  544 actual nearest-codeword decodings fail, exactly the odd-error cases.

These exhaustive small instances validate formulas and the candidate gates.
They do not replace the general derivations, instantiate an asymptotic LWE
hardness family, or prove FHE security. The diagnostic radius includes zero
to separate algebra from noise; no hardness is claimed for zero-error LWE.

Rechecking the alternative KDM formats
--------------------------------------
The existing local primary papers were inspected at their algorithm level.
BV11KDM, PDF page 7, implements quadratic KDM by a second encryption layer;
decryption has a quadratic secret contribution, consistent with the current
recursive-feature design. It is not a linear-decryption replacement.
https://www.iacr.org/archive/crypto2011/68410501/68410501.pdf
BHHI10, PDF page 6, recovers receiver randomness and then evaluates the
receiver's protocol output in its bounded-KDM construction; that is an
additional decoder, not native GSW's linear phase. Applebaum11 Proposition
5.1, PDF page 16, requires simulatable FULL KDM to obtain the public evaluator.
Its bounded amplification theorem does not supply that stronger simulator.
These checks repeat the relevant interfaces because a generic KDM theorem
does not preserve the needed ciphertext evaluation format automatically.
No new paper was needed; all 45 existing reference PDFs are retained.

Remaining construction targets after this rethink
--------------------------------------------------
The new concrete gate for any secret-representation route is an explicit
public conversion from its secure scalar controls to the actual encrypted
BITS used by the unchanged-secret decryption circuit. The conversion must
control source-error amplification, preserve the complete joint public law,
and retain reusable output margins. An arbitrary ring-polynomial conversion
and the particular coarse-body rescaler above fail these gates. A different
distribution-specific nonlinear converter with auxiliary public data is
not excluded, but none has been constructed here.

The existing direct plaintext-game route remains logically available: bound
familyControlReplacementAdvantage for the real IND-CPA observer directly by
ordinary-LWE advantages. This does not demand a full generic KDM theorem.
It still needs an actual efficient reduction generating every correlated
column, rather than a new assumption about that joint view. A joint nonlinear
table/control compiler is the other principal target; the lift above shows
that an exact full-table uniform endpoint alone is achievable while the bit
control functionality remains missing. This sharper separation is the new
evidence, not a claimed solution to either target.

No Lean source, scheme definition, assumption, import or reference changed.
The earlier 9,255-job build and focused 978-declaration audit concern the same
Lean inputs and remain applicable. The current new results are mathematical
derivations and executable finite checks. The full requested proof remains
unfinished; its scope has not been reduced to the working lifted phase view.

Python AST parsing and independent per-record/summary checks pass. All 479
Lean/config/dependency files still match the previously validated mirror;
git diff --check passes. No build was rerun because none of its Lean inputs
changed. The complete proof remains unachieved.

2026-10-18 further rethink: signed simulation and a noise-aware level hybrid
=========================================================================
The previous user-requested rethink made concrete progress: it tested the
square-zero lift and an actual public coarse-body rescaler, identifying the
bit-control and amplified-odd-error gates. This fresh request investigates a
different proof strategy, without changing the correctly refreshing recursive
family or introducing an extra cryptographic assumption. The last returned
goal status remains BLOCKED; no automatic ACTIVE transition is asserted.

The most useful new target is a level-by-level, ciphertext-distribution hybrid.
An exact signed simulation of all noiseless cubic messages has an exponential
weight problem, but the existing interval noise permits deleting low-weight
cubic columns cheaply. That deletion removes a hypothesis needed by the exact
signed lower bound, so the two calculations must not be conflated.

An explicit paired simulation, and its loss
-------------------------------------------
Let F(s) be the COMPLETE control-message vector at binary key s, with N=2^n.
Choose an independent uniform anchor r in {0,1}^n. For each output coordinate
j choose independent uniform a_j in (Z/qZ)^n and set the affine polynomial

  Q_j(s) = F_j(r) + <a_j,s-r> modulo q.

At s=r its vector value is exactly F(s). At every s!=r, one coefficient of
s-r is +1 or -1, a unit even for power-of-two q. Thus Q(s) is a uniform
vector, with its coordinates independent conditional on r. If U denotes that
uniform message-vector law and P_plus the anchor construction, then for
EVERY fixed key s the exact signed identity is

  delta_F(s) = N*P_plus(s) - (N-1)*U.

U is also obtained by choosing independent uniform affine constant terms.
Both comparison samplers use affine, hence quadratic, message maps, sampled
independently of the actual hidden key. They are efficient to sample for a
polynomial-size control table. The identity handles ALL correlated messages
at once, rather than guessing one column while leaving other cubic columns
unsimulated. In effect, the real component occurs when r equals the key.

Applying the same fresh-encryption kernel conditional on the key lifts this
identity to public-view laws: the public zero table is retained, the actual
fresh scalar masks/errors are sampled under that key, and only the control
message vector changes. Arbitrary subsequent randomized processing is linear
in these laws, including the actual adaptive encryption-game continuation.
Consequently one obtains the mathematical finite bound

  |win_F - 1/2| <= N*epsilon_plus + (N-1)*epsilon_uniform,

where each epsilon is covered by the existing quadratic-game theorem and its
explicit ordinary-LWE reductions/statistical terms. The total signed weight
is 2N-1. This is an exponential-loss identity, not a standard polynomial-loss
security reduction or a new Lean theorem. It does not establish the requested
asymptotic security. Standard negligible ordinary-LWE advantages alone do not
justify multiplying them by 2^n.

Why full-message quadratic signed mixtures cannot have polynomial weight
------------------------------------------------------------------------
Here the target includes ALL unit-weight distinct-triple monomials. Such
coordinates occur at level zero of the actual recursive control table, by
RecursiveFeatureGSW.controlMessage_quadratic with gadget weight one.
Take any fixed vector of quadratic polynomials Q(s) over Z/(2^L)Z and let S
be its agreement set with every such cubic target simultaneously.

Reduce coefficients/equalities modulo two. On S let V be the F_2-span of
1, s_i and s_i*s_j for i<j. It has at most

  D = 1+n+binomial(n,2)

generators. Multiplying any generator by a coordinate either gives a lower
degree monomial, or a cubic that is represented in V by the assumed agreement.
Thus V is closed under multiplication by every coordinate. It contains all
point indicators on S: multiply the coordinate factors s_i or 1-s_i. These
indicators span every F_2-valued function on S. Therefore |S|<=D. This is the
restricted-key-set version of the indicator argument already formalized in
GSWFeatureClosure; the restricted-set statement here is a mathematical
derivation, not a newly checked Lean declaration. The zero quadratic map
attains D agreement keys, namely keys of Hamming weight at most two.

Suppose fixed key-independent quadratic maps and signed weights w_a represent
the conditional COMPLETE message channel exactly, with sum w_a=1. Let P be
the total positive weight and M the magnitude of the total negative weight.
The target agrees with itself with probability one at a uniform key; each
quadratic branch agrees on at most D/N of keys. Since agreement probabilities
are nonnegative, negative weights can only reduce this test's signed value:

  1 <= P*D/N,       P-M=1,
  sum |w_a| = P+M >= 2N/D-1.

This is exponential in n, even allowing all quadratic maps, arbitrary
correlation among their output coordinates, and negative weights. It is a
lower bound for EXACT CONDITIONAL MESSAGE simulation with the unit-weight
coordinates included. It is NOT a lower bound for noisy ciphertext-only
approximation or an actual observer-specific reduction.

The existing branchwise quadratic source bound contains entropyBound =
sqrt((q^d-1)/2^n)/2 inside statisticalLoss/implementationLoss, as read directly
in RecursiveQuadraticParameters. With q>=2 and d>=1, independently bounding
each branch and applying the signed triangle inequality gives a contribution
at least (2N/D-1)/(2*sqrt(N)). This particular composed upper-bound expression
is already greater than 1/2 at n=10 and greater than 1 at n=16, even before
ordinary-LWE terms. It grows rather than becoming negligible. This concerns
that branchwise proof expression, not actual TV distance or adversary
advantage. Cancellation before taking absolute values could evade this
calculation, but has to be proved for the actual source/observer.

Source averaging does not give a pointwise probability kernel
--------------------------------------------------------------
For one Boolean cubic on three bits, there is an optimal signed quadratic
simulation of norm 4/3. Let Q_a equal the cubic truth table with the value at
one corner a flipped. It has even truth-table parity and hence degree at most
two over F_2. Give the uniform mixture of the eight Q_a weight 7/6 and the
uniform mixture of their complements weight -1/6. This represents the cubic
channel exactly. Every quadratic branch has agreement 1,3,5 or 7, so the
centered-agreement witness proves norm >=8/6=4/3. This small example does not
license a polynomial-loss hybrid for all columns: other real cubic columns
would still have to be simulated in each hybrid.

The actual public recursive compiler is T=A-M, linear=y-a+l, body=y0+c for a
quadratic s^T*M*s+l*s+c. At a FIXED zero source tape the sixteen branch outputs
are distinct. Each negative branch contributes mass -1/48 at its output.
Thus the signed phase-channel identity is not a positive public sampler on
every fixed source input. Averaging the complete secret-consistent source
does restore the correct nonnegative fresh-ciphertext law, as checked below.

The distinction remains relevant to the real encryption-game continuation.
At a zero public table, q=2 and one binary gadget level, encryption is bit*I
for every selector tape. A valid GSWMasking observer can read the bit and
guess correctly on the positive compiler atoms and deliberately incorrectly
on the negative atoms. The signed winning value at that fixed source input
is then 7/6. This is a signed combination, NOT a probability exceeding one.
It disproves an automatic pointwise-[0,1] claim even for an encryption-game
observer. It is not an attack on the correctly sampled real scheme, and the
small zero-error example is not an LWE-hardness instantiation. Clipping or
normalizing such a signed source test needs its own complete argument.

A positive next hybrid: delete low gadget-weight cubic columns
---------------------------------------------------------------
The unit-weight hypothesis above is avoidable at the ciphertext-law level.
The actual recursive family uses independent scalar body errors from the
finite-budget UniformInterval sampler. Compare it with a SECURITY HYBRID
whose genuine-cubic message is set to zero only at binary gadget levels
ell<t. The real generator, evaluator and correctness theorem are unchanged.

Condition on the key and every mask/other error draw. Each changed scalar
ciphertext differs only by its body message, either zero or 2^ell. Absorb that
change into its independent body error. The existing checked theorem
UniformInterval.shift_intCast_le bounds an exact interval shift by
2^ell/(2E+1). Its finite-sampler version sample_shift_le_centered adds
2*2^(-R), where R is the rejection budget. Finite-product TV and averaging
over all the retained draws keep the ENTIRE public view and its correlations.

There are C=n*(n-1)*(n-2) ordered distinct-index scalar columns per level.
For binary base two, summing the bounds gives the mathematical full-view bound

  TV(real public view, low-level-deleted public view)
    <= C*(2^t-1)/(2E+1) + 2*C*t*2^(-R).

Every arbitrary randomized continuation, including the actual adaptive
encryption game, loses at most the same quantity. The first term applies
without a modular interval injectivity assumption by the checked shift
bound. This full-view specialization has not yet been added as a Lean
theorem; its scalar/product ingredients already exist in the library.

For the CURRENT family, with a=parameter+1, n=2*a^5, E=2^a, R=a^2, choose
t=floor(a/2). The bound is a polynomial times 2^(-a/2), plus a smaller retry
term, and is negligible by the usual exponential-versus-polynomial estimate.
Explicitly, C<=8*a^15 gives an envelope
4*a^15*2^(-a/2) + 8*a^16*2^(-a^2), which is negligible.
The finite diagnostic verifies the exact interval-product TV in 18 small
instances and evaluates seven exact rational family envelopes. The envelope
is below 2^(-parameter/8) at parameters 512 and 1024; those finite examples
illustrate rather than prove the asymptotic claim.

After this deletion there are no unit-weight cubic messages in the security
hybrid. All surviving cubic weights are divisible by 2^t. Reducing their
target equations modulo two gives zero and does NOT give the previous
agreement-set hypothesis. One cannot divide by 2^t in Z/(2^L)Z, or assume
every quadratic branch coefficient is divisible by it. Thus the exponential
full-unit-message signed lower bound cannot simply be reapplied. No cheap
high-level signed simulation is asserted; it remains the next construction
question, now separated from the low-level terms that interval noise hides.

Executable evidence and next requirements
------------------------------------------
scripts/check-gsw-signed-quadratic-simulation.py writes
docs/GSWSignedQuadraticSimulationExploration.json. Its final run checks:
* the exact norm-4/3 decomposition and all 128 Boolean quadratic maps;
* the fixed-source negative atom -1/48 and 32 actual bit/challenge checks
  giving the signed GSW game value 7/6;
* 524,288 actual source/compiler branch cases, with all 65,536 resulting
  ciphertext probabilities matching the positive averaged cubic law;
* five exact paired full-message channel instances, including the complete
  four-cubic vector at n=4, with 496 joint channel entries and 303,616 free
  affine-coefficient evaluations;
* all 65,792 subsets of the binary n=3,4 key spaces for simultaneous quadratic
  interpolation of ALL cubics; their maximum sizes are exactly 7 and 11;
* eight signed-norm/branchwise-entropy scaling cases, 18 exact interval-TV
  checks, and seven rational low-level-deletion family envelopes.

The next constructive target is now specific: first formalize the complete
low-level-deletion view comparison, then find a joint ordinary-LWE comparison
for the remaining nonunit-weight noisy cubic ciphertexts. A successful
signed approach must preserve cancellations at the ciphertext or actual
plaintext-game level and produce a bounded efficient source test; exact
noiseless message representation followed by branchwise absolute bounds
has the exponential loss above. A genuinely nonlinear positive simulator
for the same remaining view is another way to discharge that target.

None of these calculations proves that high-weight cancellation exists,
that the original family is insecure, or that ordinary-LWE-only FHE is
impossible. No independent KDM/circular assumption or changed key schedule
has been added. No Lean source or reference changed in this rethink; the
45 existing PDFs suffice for this algebraic investigation. The new signed
identities, agreement argument and full-view low-level specialization are
mathematical derivations with executable finite checks, not newly checked
Lean declarations. The full requested security proof remains unfinished.

weights, entropy-expression floors and the concrete low-level envelopes
pass. All 479 authoritative Lean/config/dependency files still match the

2026-10-18 -- CHECKED COMPLETE-VIEW LOW-LEVEL CUBIC DELETION

This continuation implements the positive step isolated in the preceding
signed-simulation rethink. The original requested theorem remains
ordinary-LWE-only security of repeatably bootstrappable GSW under one
unchanged secret. No independent circular/KDM hypothesis, fresh key
schedule, or replacement of the actual correctly refreshing scheme is
introduced. The goal remains active after resumption and unfinished.

New module: FormalProof4FHE/LWE/RecursiveFeatureLowLevelHybrid.lean.
The materialized deletedView retains the actual public encryption table,
every public mask, every scalar error draw, and every control outside the
selected set. For each genuine distinct-index cubic control with gadget
level ell < cutoff, it replaces only the encrypted message by zero.
The same binary secret generates the entire view. The definition is a
security hybrid; it is not substituted into the scheme/evaluator.

The exact identity publicView_shiftDraws_deletion proves that translating
only the corresponding body error by minus the original message produces
this independently defined full hybrid. Reindexing the actual finite IID
error sampler charges only those body coordinates. The full public view,
including arbitrary key-dependent correlations between controls and the
public encryption table, is preserved throughout the comparison.
No independent-control marginal or one-column-only security claim is used.
The scalar message on a binary key is either zero or its gadget weight.
UniformInterval.sample_shift_le_centered and the centered natural-cast
bound therefore charge a selected level ell by

    base^ell/(2*radius+1) + 2*(1/2)^rounds.

The exact finite sum of these selected column charges is deletionLoss.
keyView_tvDist_deleted_le bounds the complete public-view total variation
by this quantity for ANY secret sampler. deleted_observation_tvDist_le
extends it through any common randomized continuation; in particular it
covers adaptive message choice after inspecting the entire public view.
deleted_controls_retained certifies equality of each retained stored
ciphertext, and the cutoff-zero deletion loss is exactly zero.

For the actual family, let a=parameter+1, n=2*a^5, levels=a^2,
radius=2^a, rounds=a^2, and cutoff=floor(a/2). The following are now
checked Lean results, rather than only executable finite diagnostics:

* family_scalar_cost_le: the coarse per-column charge is at most
  4*(3/4)^parameter;
* family_deletionLoss_le: the full charge is at most
  4*n*(rows(n)*levels)*(3/4)^parameter;
* family_deletionLoss_negligible: ENNReal.ofReal of the full charge is
  negligible in the standard polynomial-times-probability definition.

The polynomial factor is deliberately a coarse count of every control.
The sharper ordered-distinct-triple geometric sum derived previously,
C*(2^cutoff-1)/(2*radius+1)+2*C*cutoff*2^(-rounds), is still a mathematical
bound, not the closed-form counting theorem checked in this module.
The implemented exact finite-sum charge is sufficient for negligibility.

controlReplacement_le_deletionLoss_add splits the old real-to-quadratic
comparison into the negligible deletion charge plus the explicit
experiment deletedReplacementAdvantage. The latter compares the FULL
truncated view to the existing complete quadratic simulator under the
actual public-key encryption game observer. The new
family_oneTime_abs_signedAdvantage_le composes this split with the
existing ordinary-LWE reduction, implementation loss, and masking loss.
It is a partial security bound, not a discharge of the surviving term.

The remaining question is now narrower: can the surviving nonunit-weight
noisy cubic controls be jointly simulated/reduced from ordinary LWE? The
previous mod-two/unit-weight signed-message lower bound does not directly
apply after deleting the low levels, but its failure to apply is not a
positive simulation theorem. Dividing a high-level message by its gadget
weight is not a valid ring operation on an arbitrary quadratic branch or
the full ciphertext. A cancellation argument must act on the full noisy
ciphertext law and furnish an efficient bounded ordinary-source test.
Alternatively, a positive nonlinear source simulator must match this
full truncated law and satisfy the already checked uniform full-table
marginal endpoint. Neither simulator is currently constructed.

No new primary reference was needed for this formal specialization.

package/test build succeeds (9,256 jobs). The focused GSW axiom audit also
succeeds with -DwarningAsError=true, covering 1,004 distinct declarations,
including all 26 new theorems. Every focused axiom set is a subset of
{propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound}; there are no warnings, errors,
sorryAx dependencies, or new proof placeholders. All 480 authoritative
Lean/config/dependency files match the validated mirror byte for byte.
git diff --check passes. Build and audit logs are respectively
/tmp/fhe-gsw-low-level-build.log and /tmp/fhe-gsw-low-level-audit.log.
No build or experiment remains pending. This validation covers the new

higher-level replacement or the full requested ordinary-LWE FHE theorem.


2026-10-18 -- NONUNIT CUBIC AGREEMENT: TOP-BIT CAPACITY AND AN EXPLICIT GAP

The previous goal turn made concrete progress: the complete public-view
low-level deletion, its negligible family loss, and its composition into
the actual IND-CPA bound are checked in Lean. The present continuation
investigates whether the surviving nonunit weights evade the exponential
loss of exact signed quadratic-message simulation. It neither changes
the requested theorem nor adds a circular/KDM assumption.

A tempting extension of the old unit-weight agreement bound is FALSE.
There are five binary secret coordinates, D=1+5+binomial(5,2)=16 quadratic
features, and modulus 8. Let T be ANY triple of the five coordinates,
let U be its two-coordinate complement, and define

 Q_T(s) = 2*(sum over ALL pairs s_i*s_j
              + sum over pairs contained in T s_i*s_j
              + product over U s_i), modulo 8.

For every odd-Hamming-weight key and for the zero key,
Q_T(s)=4*product over T s_i, simultaneously for ALL ten triples T.
Thus the same quadratic vector agrees with the half-modulus-weight
cubic vector on 17 keys, strictly exceeding D=16. The corresponding
modulus-16 and modulus-32 examples multiply these quadratic coefficients
by two and four. The new executable checks the explicit formulas directly
on all keys/triples (960 formula evaluations), independently of its
modular interpolation solver. The old unit-weight D bound must NOT be
silently applied after the checked low-level deletion.

There is, however, a different weighted agreement obstruction. Fix any
agreement set S, q=2^k, a nonzero weight g=2^ell with ell<k, and a fixed
quadratic Q_T for every triple. Let M be the additive image of the
D-coefficient quadratic evaluation map into (Z/q)^S. Its subgroup
M[2]={m in M:2*m=0} contains at most 2^D elements. For a finite abelian
group, the kernel of doubling and the quotient by doubles have the same
cardinality. A surjection from (Z/q)^D to M induces a surjection on those
quotients; this proves the stated capacity without wrongly asserting
that individual order-two elements lift to order-two coefficients.

Every binary degree-at-most-two evaluation pattern has its top-bit
(q/2)-multiple in M directly. Every degree-three monomial also does,
since multiplying g*cubic=Q_T on S by 2^(k-1-ell) gives
(q/2)*cubic in M. This multiplies an equality and does not divide
quadratic coefficients by the nonunit g. Top-bit encoding is injective
and turns XOR into addition, so the whole binary degree-at-most-three
evaluation space V3(S) embeds into M[2]. Therefore

    H3(S) = dim_F2 V3(S) <= D.

If S shatters d secret coordinates, restricting to representatives of
all 2^d coordinate patterns makes the degree-at-most-three monomials on
those coordinates independent. Consequently

    1+d+binomial(d,2)+binomial(d,3) <= H3(S) <= D.

Sauer--Shelah then bounds |S| by sum_{j<=d} binomial(n,j). The applicable
standard result is present in the current mathlib source at
Mathlib/Combinatorics/SetFamily/Shatter.lean, specifically
Finset.card_le_card_shatterer and Finset.card_shatterer_le_sum_vcDim.
The official source documentation was checked at
https://leanprover-community.github.io/mathlib4_docs/Mathlib/Combinatorics/SetFamily/Shatter.html.
No additional PDF was needed.

For D=1+n+binomial(n,2), the displayed cubic inequality gives
d=O(n^(2/3)). Hence every fixed quadratic vector agrees with all weighted
cubics on at most 2^(O(n^(2/3)*log n)) of the 2^n keys. This is a weaker
agreement bound than the former unit-weight bound, but is sufficient to
force exponential total weight in any exact signed representation of the
noiseless conditional message channel. If B is the binomial-sum agreement
upper bound, the agreement-event witness and normalization sum w_i=1 give
positive weight >=2^n/B, and therefore total absolute weight

    L >= 2^(n+1)/B - 1 = 2^(n-o(n)).

For example, the exact integer/rational envelope at n=4096 gives
L>2^2312, independently of which surviving gadget level is used. There
is at least one surviving level whenever the cutoff is below k. The
production cutoff floor((parameter+1)/2) is below k=(parameter+1)^2.
Thus merely removing unit-weight controls does not rescue exact signed
noiseless message representation followed by branchwise absolute bounds.
This is not a lower bound on adversarial advantage, noisy public-view
statistical distance, or any bounded source test retaining cancellations.

Formal scope: new module FormalProof4FHE/LWE/GSWWeightedCubic.lean proves
13 algebraic results: finite doubling kernel/quotient equality; monotonicity
under surjections; the exact two-torsion alternatives for an even modulus;
the 2^D capacity of a D-coefficient image; injective top-bit encoding and
XOR additivity; top-bit membership from a weighted message; and the
resulting cardinality/dimension bounds for independently represented
binary evaluation patterns. These are checked Lean theorems. The full
application to all cubic monomials, the shattering argument, asymptotic
agreement estimate and signed-norm witness above are mathematical
derivations with executable finite checks, not yet an end-to-end Lean
obstruction theorem. None is a full FHE security theorem.

Executable: scripts/check-gsw-weighted-cubic-agreement.py.
Artifact: docs/GSWWeightedCubicAgreementExploration.json.
The modular solver uses invertible row/column operations over Z/(2^k),
choosing the least 2-adic valuation pivot and checking divisibility for
all RHS columns. Its feasibility answers are checked independently
against exhaustive coefficient enumeration for 270,400 matrix/RHS cases;
exact order-two-image cardinalities are checked for all 4,432 matrices.
There are 134,264 further agreement-subset checks: every subset for the
3- and 4-bit cases, plus deterministic random and parity-slice cases on
5 and 6 bits. Every feasible case satisfies H3(S)<=the image's socle
rank<=D, the shattering lower bound, and the Sauer--Shelah cardinality
bound. The two 17-key nonunit counterexamples are among these cases.

The next security target must still operate on the complete noisy
ciphertext/game law and preserve cancellations before taking absolute
values, or give a positive nonlinear full-view source simulator. The new
capacity argument concerns the hidden-key conditional noiseless channel;
it does not rule out either of these routes. Neither bounded efficient
source test nor compatible positive simulator has been constructed, so
the full ordinary-LWE-only security objective remains active and open.

Validation: the new module compiles with -DwarningAsError=true and all
13 new theorems are imported and included in the focused audit. The full
package/test build succeeds (9,257 jobs). The focused audit succeeds with
-DwarningAsError=true for 1,017 distinct declarations, with all axiom
sets subsets of {propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound}. No warnings,
errors, sorryAx dependencies, or new proof placeholders occur. All 481
Lean/config/dependency files match the validated mirror. The executable
exits zero; Python AST, artifact histogram counts, exact rational norm
envelopes, and the explicit counterexample formulas pass independent
consistency checks. git diff --check passes. The build/audit logs are
/tmp/fhe-gsw-weighted-cubic-build.log and
/tmp/fhe-gsw-weighted-cubic-audit.log. All initiated processes are terminal.
The original goal is still active; these are partial algebraic and
obstruction results, not a completed ordinary-LWE security proof.


2026-10-18 -- SIGNED SOURCE CANCELLATION: L2 CONTRACTION AND CLIPPING GAP

The preceding goal turn was progress: it added the checked top-bit capacity
algebra and new weighted-agreement evidence. The current continuation tests
the remaining possibility that cancellation after hidden-key/source
averaging avoids exponential branchwise absolute bounds. It preserves the
full original ordinary-LWE-only, same-secret repeatable-GSW objective.

There is a positive mathematical identity for the paired-anchor signed
kernel. Let s range over the N=2^n binary keys, let psi(s) contain the
linear secret coordinates (and optionally the quadratic features), and
let F(s) be ANY vector of m target messages in Z/q. Coefficients consist
of A in (Z/q)^(m*d) and c in (Z/q)^m. Sample an anchor r uniformly and
A uniformly, and set c=F(r)-A*psi(r). Write P_F for this coefficient law,
U for uniform coefficients, and K_F=N*P_F-(N-1)*U. Its total signed
mass is one. Averaging compiler branches conditionally on any key gives
the exact target message channel, as checked previously.

At Fourier frequency (eta, omega) on (A,c), averaging A kills every
anchor except those satisfying

    eta[j,i] = omega[j]*psi(r)[i], for all j,i.

If omega has a nonzero component, at most one binary r satisfies these
equalities: two different keys differ by +/-1 in some linear coordinate,
and multiplying that difference by the nonzero component stays nonzero.
This uniqueness does not require omega[j] to be a unit. If compatible,
the Fourier multiplier of K_F is the unit phase character(omega*F(r));
otherwise it is zero. The constant multiplier is one. Thus all multipliers
have modulus at most one, with exactly 1+N*(q^m-1) nonzero modes.
The squared multiplier is independent of F and is an idempotent 0/1
projection. Equivalently K_F^*K_F is the zero-target projection K_0.
Finite Parseval gives the genuine cancellation-preserving bound

    E_uniform |K_F*f|^2 <= E_uniform |f|^2.

This is a mathematical derivation, not a newly checked Lean theorem.
The new executable verifies every Fourier mode in eight affine/quadratic,
scalar/vector examples over q=2 and q=4, including nonunit-weight target
cubics: 88,720 exact modes. Gaussian integer character sums are computed
with roots only in {1,-1,i,-i}; no trigonometric approximation is used,
and every intermediate is an exactly represented bounded integer.
It also checks mean preservation and the squared-norm contraction with a
bounded observer chosen to detect the positive part of the signed kernel.
Collecting duplicate coefficient atoms reduces the crude 2N-1 branch
weight in several examples, but does not eliminate negative atoms.

The key obstacle is not resolved by this L2 identity. Its uniform-source
norm bound neither makes the signed score pointwise a probability nor
shows it efficiently evaluable using polynomially many compiler/game
queries. It also gives no real-source clipping-error bound. In particular,
clipping into [0,1] cannot simply be declared a lossless conversion.

The executable checks this explicitly on the earlier optimal 16-branch
quadratic stencil for one three-bit cubic over Z/2, with weights +7/48
and -1/48. Let f select its eight positive coefficient atoms. The signed
source score has the following exact distribution over the 128 relevant
coefficient profiles:

    score 7/6: one profile; -1/6: one; 0: seventy;
          7/24: twenty-eight; -1/24: twenty-eight.

Its Fourier multiplier magnitude is at most one, yet its uniform clipping
bias is 7/768. Thus even a uniform L2 contraction does not imply zero or
negligible clipping bias at a fixed instance.

This is connected to the actual RecursiveQuadratic source compiler,
T=A-M, c=y-a+l, b=z+o, rather than just an arbitrary signed vector.
The observed profile is (b,c_0,c_1,c_2,T_01,T_02,T_12). Exhausting all
65,536 uniform source tapes gives each profile 512 times. Exhausting
all 32,768 real zero-error binary-key/source tapes gives:

    real signed mean = actual averaged cubic event probability = 35/512;
    uniform signed mean = 1/16;
    real clipped mean = 941/12288;
    uniform clipped mean = 55/768.

Consequently the true signed source gap is 3/512, while the clipped
source gap is 61/12288, smaller by 11/12288. The real clipping bias is
101/12288; the uniform clipping bias is 7/768. Averaging the real source
restores the correct positive target-law expectation but does not make
clipping preserve its comparison with the uniform source.

This bounded observer is also a valid GSWMasking game continuation in the
small zero-public-table experiment: p=0, q=2, base=2, one level. Encryption
of the selected challenge bit is bit*I. Read a diagonal entry; guess it
on the chosen public-context event and flip it outside that event. The
winning indicator equals f, checked on all 256 context/bit pairs. The
other stored controls can be ignored by this observer. These are small,
zero-error diagnostics, not an attack on the production family and not
a proof that its asymptotic clipping error is large.

Executable: scripts/check-gsw-signed-source-cancellation.py.
Artifact: docs/GSWSignedSourceCancellationExploration.json.
No Lean input or reference PDF changes in this continuation.

The remaining constructive requirements are still a polynomial-time
bounded ordinary-source test preserving the actual full-view advantage,
including its real-source range/error control, or a positive nonlinear
simulator matching the correctly refreshing view and meeting the checked
uniform full-table marginal condition. No such test or simulator has
been instantiated. Formalizing the L2 algebra alone would not discharge
these requirements. The finite clipping witness rules out the proposed
lossless clipping shortcut, not every corrected clipping strategy.
