2026-10-19 -- BOOLEAN POLYNOMIAL COMPILERS OF TOTAL DEGREE THREE TO FIVE
======================================================================

The newly approved plan is implemented in
  scripts/search-gsw-boolean-polynomial-compiler.py
with an independently replayable study artifact
  docs/GSWBooleanPolynomialCompilerExploration.json.
This is a new bounded construction search, not completion of the full
ordinary-LWE-only same-secret security objective. Earlier blocked audits
remain historical evidence; this attempt neither assumes their missing
terms negligible nor restarts the old lattice/MaxSMT queries unchanged.

Candidate class and exact supported certificate
------------------------------------------------
Join three independent source mask blocks into A and write y=A^T*s+e.
For D=3,4,5, the mask-only builder chooses coefficients of

  P(s,y) = sum_{|U|<=2, |U|+|nu|<=D} theta[U,nu] * s_U * y^nu.

The runtime compiler receives only these coefficients, public source bodies,
and public dimensions/modulus. Its complete output has b=P_empty(y),
c_i=P_{i}(y), T_{i,j}=-P_{i,j}(y) for i<j, and zero remaining T entries.
An independent ordinary-source recursive zero is then added, retaining all
coordinates and the separately materialized full public table. The complete
supported control error includes the zero source's additional (n+1)*E.
No secret, source error or private correctness oracle is a compiler input.

Expand P(s,A^T*s+e)-w*s_i*s_j*s_k using integer binomial multiplication
and s_i^2=s_i. Every coefficient of error degree one or two is required
to vanish modulo q. These are Hasse coefficients: there is no factorial
division or field assumption at the power-of-two modulus. All remaining
coefficients are lifted to the centered interval [-q/2,q/2).

Let r_U be the zero-error coefficients. With chi_K(s)=prod_{i in K}(2*s_i-1),
their exact rational Walsh coefficients are

  h_K = sum_{U superset K} r_U / 2^|U|.

For residual coefficients r[U,beta] at error order |beta|>=3, use

  J = ceil(sum_K |h_K|
           + sum_{|beta|>=3} E^|beta| * sum_U |r[U,beta]|).

This bounds the actual modular phase error for every Boolean key and every
supported source-error vector. Rational division here is only in the
integer/rational error analysis, never division by two in ZMod q. The
integer optimization clears denominators by multiplying the objective by
2^D. A strict improvement must lower the INTEGER bound J, not merely its
unrounded score.

The new freedom is real but not yet a useful secure construction. For example,
for s^2=s and y=s+e the exact degree-four identity is

  y^4 - 2*s*y^3 + 2*s*y - s = 2*s*e^3 + e^4.

The third-order error coefficient depends on s, unlike the earlier constant
cubic tensor. This is a zero-message identity, not a cubic encryption or a
hard source. The study distinguishes a genuinely degree-four/five program
from a solver run at degree four/five that merely finds a better cubic.
The unrestricted degree-three comparison also permits repeated source
columns, beyond the earlier tensor restricted to one column per block.

A separate exact positive-message check uses the structured source y=s+e.
The public quadratic-in-s polynomial

  P4 = y0^2*y1*y2 - s0*y1*y2 - s1*y0^2*y2 - s2*y0^2*y1
       + s0*s1*y2 + s0*s2*y1 + s1*s2*y0^2

has phase s0*s1*s2 + 2*s0*e0*e1*e2 + e0^2*e1*e2. All 864
Boolean-key/ternary-error cases at q=2,4,8,32 pass direct full-coordinate
checks, independent certificate replay and residual-coordinate round trips.
Its supported bound 2*E^3+E^4 is worse than the cubic identity's E^3 on that
same source. The source mask is the identity, not a hard random LWE mask.
This verifies expressiveness of the new class, not an advancement-gate win.

An exact lower bound saves provably fruitless certificate queries
-----------------------------------------------------------------
For THIS certificate and candidate class,

  J >= min(E^3, ceil(w/8)).

If any positive-error coefficient is nonzero, its contribution is at least
E^3. Otherwise all positive-error coefficients vanish, since orders one and
two are constrained to zero. In the Boolean polynomial ring, the invertible
translation e=y-A^T*s then says P(s,y) is independent of y. Set y=0: the
result is quadratic in s. Its discrepancy from the targeted distinct cubic
therefore has cubic coefficient -w and Walsh coefficient -w/8. The absolute
Walsh sum is at least w/8. Combining the cases proves the displayed bound.
This argument needs no field, invertible source mask, key knowledge, or
asymptotic geometric assumption. It is a mathematical derivation with exact
executable consistency checks, not a newly formalized Lean theorem.

When the incumbent meets this lower bound, the record is marked
certificate_lower_bound and no strict-improvement solver is called. The
statement excludes improvement of this conservative certificate only; it
does not lower-bound every actual error distribution or rule out other
compilers, noise analyses, or full ordinary-LWE reductions.

Solver implementation and retained pilot
-----------------------------------------
An initial coefficient-parameterized pilot was preserved after 223 seconds.
It returned six unknown results, one wall timeout and nine lower-bound skips,
with no accepted improvement. One additional in-flight query was interrupted
while changing the encoding, not declared infeasible. Its complete checkpoint
is retained inside the final artifact as prior_study, including its original
script hash. Temporary source/log snapshots are under
/tmp/fhe-boolean-polynomial-pilot.{py,json,log}.

The main solver instead chooses residual coefficients directly. Let R(s,e)
contain the arbitrary zero-error discrepancy and error orders at least three.
Form

  Q(s,y) = w*s_i*s_j*s_k + R(s,y-A^T*s).

Require every coefficient of Q with secret degree greater than two to be zero
modulo q; the remaining coefficients materialize the public P. This is an
equivalent parameterization of the same exact class. It substantially reduces
the linear integer system and makes the low-error variables explicit. The
candidate is re-expanded in the FORWARD direction after extraction, and its
residual must match every modeled coefficient. No solver model is accepted
from its status or objective value alone. A valid improving model returned
with unknown status is retained, with optimality explicitly unproved.

The installed isolated solver is Z3 4.13.3. It is not added to project/global
dependencies. Saved-artifact replay uses only the Python standard library.
The generation default caps each query at 30 seconds and the combined pilot
plus main study at 1,200 seconds, counting construction and solver work.
No new optimizer starts after that deadline. Baseline validation/finalization
may finish afterward; elapsed_seconds records the full current run, while
config records its remaining search allowance and the charged pilot time.
Each worker owns its child process; deadline, normal completion and missing
result paths all close/join it. No unrelated process is killed.

The fixed grid is (n,m)=(3,1),(4,1),(6,1),(3,2), bit lengths 12 and 24,
radii 2^ceil(sqrt(bits)),1,3, and degrees 3,4,5. There is one public mask draw
per case, with seed 20261020+100000*n+1000*m+bits, and no favorable resampling.
The coefficient cap is 600. Top levels are screened across cases/radii before
middle and lowest levels, always targeting the first distinct triple. Limits,
unstarted queries and rejected/unavailable models are recorded separately.

Only an independently checked improvement using an actual program term of
total degree above three enters full-table work. For the first winning case
in configuration order, all distinct triples and ALL gadget levels receive
validated selected certificates, even if the deadline leaves only baselines
for remaining slots. Coverage is separate from optimizer completion. The
whole-table budget uses its worst J and the added source-zero error; local
improvement alone never counts as a reusable whole-table pass.

The selected toy grid cannot satisfy the existing refresh budget even with
J=0: already at n=3, bits=24, E=1 the added zero-source error gives a refresh
bound 26,041,533,333,504, exceeding q=16,777,216. The radius-one lower budget
fails in all eight configurations, and larger radii only increase it. Thus
the grid measures algebraic/local certificate improvements and coverage;
it cannot establish useful cryptographic parameters or a positive family
success probability. This is not a lower bound for the asymptotic family.

Independent verification and completion gate
---------------------------------------------
The generator uses Boolean symbolic multiplication. Replay instead fixes each
Boolean key, expands each source factor by univariate integer convolution,
and uses Boolean Mobius inversion to reconstruct the coefficient identities.
It independently averages the zero-error truth table to reconstruct rational
Walsh coefficients and bounds. Complete T,c,b coordinates are reconstructed
directly, including the independent zero and every retained public-table
coordinate. Validation witnesses retain all source equations and supported
errors under the SAME key, with deterministic RNG replay.

For these tiny keys, source recoverability is measured by exhaustive public
enumeration of all supported-error-compatible Boolean keys. This is explicitly
a finite diagnostic, not a polynomial-time source-family attack or a new
hardness assumption. All fixtures are toy sources; the three-column public
tables are not a production masking-parameter instantiation.

The required production gate remains a proved family success probability for
a specified efficient coefficient algorithm, compatible reusable noise and
the full ordinary-LWE reduction for that SAME sampler, including the existing
ordinary-to-binary source losses and reduction closure. No general-purpose
solver optimum, small-instance recovery flag, finite identity, or newly named
advantage term substitutes for those requirements. No production Lean source,
sampler, assumption or security interface is changed by this exploration.

  python3 -B scripts/search-gsw-boolean-polynomial-compiler.py --self-test
  python3 -B scripts/search-gsw-boolean-polynomial-compiler.py --verify-runner

The saved main run additionally supplies --prior-study with the preserved pilot
JSON. That pilot is embedded in the artifact, so final replay is portable and
needs no temporary pilot file. New runs without it start a fresh bounded study;
the historical interrupted queries are not silently omitted from this result.

2026-10-19 -- COMPILERS WITH BOUNDED FIRST- AND SECOND-ORDER ERRORS
================================================================

The approved relaxation plan is implemented in
  scripts/search-gsw-bounded-error-compiler.py
with a separately replayable artifact
  docs/GSWBoundedErrorCompilerExploration.json.
The old Boolean polynomial script/artifact are immutable, SHA-256-pinned
regression inputs. This implements a bounded construction experiment;
it does not complete the ordinary-LWE-only same-secret FHE security goal.
No extra hardness, key schedule or private/public trapdoor is introduced.

Candidate class and certificate
-------------------------------
As before, join three ordinary-source blocks into a public matrix A and
write y=A^T*s+e for the SAME hidden binary secret. The public mask-only
builder chooses coefficients of P(s,y) of total degree D=3 or 4, quadratic
in s. Runtime compilation evaluates public body polynomials only and
returns every T,c,b coordinate, with original-key phase

  b+s.c-s^T*T*s = P(s,y).

An independent ordinary-source recursive zero adds its actual bounded
error, at most (n+1)*E. The separately materialized three-column toy public
table is retained pointwise. This toy table is NOT substituted for the
full production sample count or its complete joint simulation obligations.
Oracle secrets/errors appear only in validation witnesses, never builder
or runtime compiler inputs.

Expand R(s,e)=P(s,A^T*s+e)-w*s_i*s_j*s_k, using Boolean reduction s_i^2=s_i
and integer binomial multiplication. Unlike the preceding search, error
orders one and two need not vanish. Lift all residual coefficients r[U,beta]
to the centered integer interval before applying rational analysis. For
chi_K(s)=product_{i in K}(2*s_i-1), let

  h[K,beta] = sum_{U superset K} r[U,beta]/2^|U|,
  J = ceil(sum_beta E^|beta| * sum_K |h[K,beta]|).

This is a supported bound for every Boolean key and |e_j|<=E, since each
chi_K has absolute value one. The Walsh transform is applied to EVERY
error coefficient polynomial, not just the zero-error term. Its positive-
error part never exceeds the preceding raw-monomial l1 bound by the
triangle inequality. Rational division occurs only in error analysis;
there is no modular division or field/factorial assumption at q=2^bits.
The integer optimizer clears denominators by multiplying by 2^D.

Residual coordinates include ALL |U|+|beta|<=D, including |U|>2. Invert
formally by e=y-A^T*s and require every coefficient of secret degree >2
in w*s_i*s_j*s_k+R(s,y-A^T*s) to vanish modulo q. The resulting polynomial
is in the original output grammar. Applying its secret-degree-two limit
to residual coordinates would be wrong: the genuine quartic example
P=s_1*s_2*y_0^2 on y_0=s_0+e_0 has order-one coefficient
2*s_0*s_1*s_2 and order-two coefficient s_1*s_2. The forward and inverse
implementations retain this degree-three secret support correctly.

Why this relaxation is different
--------------------------------
For the structured source A=diag(128,1,1), q=256, E=2, w=128, the public
program P=s_1*s_2*y_0 has phase

  w*s_0*s_1*s_2 + s_1*s_2*e_0.

Its certificate is J=2, whereas every preceding exact-first/second-
cancellation certificate has J>=min(E^3,ceil(w/8))=8. All 1,000 direct
Boolean-key/supported-error full-coordinate phase cases pass. This proves
that the relaxed certificate class can strictly improve the old one; the
structured source exposes information about its key and is NOT a hard
ordinary-source instance or a random-mask study success.

The stronger all-layer Walsh analysis is also exercised on the zero-message
identity

  y^4-y^3-2*s*y^3+3*s*y^2-s*y = (2*s-1)*e^3+e^4,
  y=s+e, s^2=s.

When included against a unit cubic target at q=256,E=2, the new certificate
is 25 instead of the old certificate's 41. This is an analysis fixture,
not a newly discovered hard-source compiler.

Exact floors, pruning and avoiding repeated queries
---------------------------------------------------
A nonzero Boolean polynomial with INTEGER coefficients has a nonzero
integer value at some Boolean input: Boolean Mobius inversion makes its
monomial representation unique. Its Walsh l1 norm bounds its supremum,
and therefore is at least one. A nonzero error layer of order k thus
contributes at least E^k to the unrounded certificate. If all error layers
vanish, invert the translation e=y-A^T*s and set y=0. The compiler is then
quadratic in s, and its cubic discrepancy has Walsh coefficient -w/8.
Consequently the relaxed class has

  J >= min(E,ceil(w/8)).

Queries requiring a nonzero first/second-order residual have J>=E. These
are bounds on THIS supported certificate, not adversarial advantages or
all possible compiler/error analyses. They are mathematical derivations
with executable consistency checks, not new Lean theorems.

For a query J<=L, all error layers with E^k>L must be zero; their variables
are removed exactly. If this leaves error degree at most k, inverse
translation gives body degree at most k. Since the output has secret
degree at most two, its total degree is at most k+2. In three planned
cases k=1, so degree four has exactly the same feasible set as degree
three, with the same objective, target and budget. An unresolved degree-
three query is referenced once as equivalent_query_reused. It remains
unresolved; no infeasibility is inferred from the equivalence.

For the six-dimensional case k=2, so degree four remains a genuinely
larger query and is run separately. Additional exact checks compare the
linear-body grammars at both degrees for four dimension/width choices.
The negative control n=4,bits=64,E=256 has J_cap=98<E and is rejected
without starting any solver. The implementation does not retain the old
E^3 floor for pruning relaxed queries.

Budget and predetermined study
--------------------------------
The new public feasibility gate uses the documented CONSERVATIVE 4*F
bound rather than silently reusing the preceding toy F-only flag. Write

  rows=n^2+n+1, growth=rows*bits,
  lookup=least power of two at least 8*rows,
  F=lookup^2*rows*growth*(growth+1)^2,
  J_cap=floor((q-1)/(4*F))-(n+1)*E.

The implementation and an independent arithmetic checker verify
4*F*(J+(n+1)*E)<q, its integer floor equivalent, and the fresh-encryption
bound against the associated output bound. Actual ordinary-source sample
counts and the full production family still require their joint proof.

The fixed cases, each with one unrejected/unresampled mask draw, are:

  n  m  bits   E    J_cap     old certificate floor at top weight
  3  1   64   256    36480      16777216

2026-10-20 -- CONSTRUCTIVE LINEAR-ERROR CHARACTER COMPILERS AND LEAN PROOFS
========================================================================

The preceding goal turn was progress: it implemented the relaxed polynomial

A tractable subfamily of the relaxed class
-----------------------------------------
Let A be the joined three-block ordinary-source mask, M=3*m its column count,
y=A^T*s+e, and chi_i(s)=2*s_i-1. For one public coefficient per unordered
secret pair and source column, form

  P_raw(s,y)=sum_{i<k,j} u[i,k,j]*chi_i(s)*chi_k(s)*y_j.

This is quadratic in s and linear in public bodies, of total degree three.
Its noiseless cubic coefficient on a distinct triple I is

  4*sum_{i<k in I,j} u[i,k,j]*A[I minus {i,k},j].

All lower-degree noiseless coefficients can be removed by a PUBLIC quadratic
correction computed from A and u alone. At gadget weight w=2^level, level>=2,
define the matrix C(A) with rows indexed by triples, columns by (i,k,j), and
entry A[r,j] when {i,k,r} is that triple, zero otherwise. Solve exactly

  C(A)*u = (w/4)*e_I mod (q/4).

Division is of integer modulus/weight, never division by four inside ZMod q.
Multiplication back by four gives the desired congruence modulo q. Lowest
levels use existing certified quadratic approximations instead. This is a
restricted constructive subfamily, not an equivalent solver for every
bounded-error polynomial or every degree-four query.

With the lower correction applied, the actual original-key phase is

  w*s_I + sum_{i<k,j} u[i,k,j]*chi_i(s)*chi_k(s)*e_j.

Each character product is plus or minus one on a Boolean key, so the actual
supported compiler error is bounded by

  J = E*sum_{i<k,j}|u[i,k,j]|.

The independent recursive zero contributes its additional (n+1)*E. This
source error is linear, not cubic, and its secret-dependent coefficients
are exactly the quadratic Walsh characters. It therefore offers an explicit
construction within the newly relaxed class rather than another unchanged
SMT timeout. It does not prove public coefficient shortness for hard sources.

New Lean construction and proofs

  FormalProof4FHE/LWE/GSWLinearCharacterCompiler.lean

(2*s_i-1)*(2*s_j-1)*z. The lower-degree correction is a concrete quadratic
polynomial of the publicly projected mask a=A*u. On an idempotent secret,
its exact residual is

  4*s_i*s_j*sum_{r notin {i,j}} a_r*s_r.

Summing proves phase_real for every binary/idempotent key and every actual
source-error vector. sourceError_bound proves the modular centered error
bound using actual public coefficient norms and supported errors only.
No cancellation or family shortness premise is silently assumed.

actual canonical public compiler. phase_error_bound_with_zero includes the

2026-10-20 -- JOINT ORDINARY-SOURCE REDUCTION FOR MATERIALIZED CUBIC CONTROLS
=========================================================================

The preceding state-check turn was NO PROGRESS toward the proof: it confirmed

The earlier signed-trace attack applies to its stated quadratic preimage

One actual joint construction
-----------------------------
  FormalProof4FHE/LWE/GSWLinearCharacterSecurity.lean
  FormalProof4FHETest/GSWLinearCharacterSecuritySmoke.lean

Let the original binary-key dimension be n, retained source width M, public
table width p and number of compiled controls k. Use ONE ordinary source
with M+(p+k)*(n+1) columns, enumerated by RecursiveQuadratic.columnsEquiv.
It supplies a common retained transcript (A,y), p fresh recursive zeros
for the public table, and k fresh recursive zeros Z_t, all under the SAME
original key. The independent masks/errors occupy the indicated columns of
one source draw; no control receives its own independently sampled base.

For a public mask-only builder u_t(A), eagerly materialize

  C_t = Z_t + compileCanonical(first,second,A,u_t(A),y).

The public view retains its complete original linear transcript (A,y), the
whole feature-coordinate public table and ALL k controls C_t simultaneously.
The private output of keygen is just the original source secret. The actual
stored public-key encryptor and recursive-feature decryptor are used in the
new AsymmEncAlg scheme. There is no auxiliary trapdoor, hidden conditional
coin sampler, independent secret schedule or assumed cubic KDM experiment.

The public transformation from (table,(source,Z)) to (table,(source,C)) has
an explicit inverse: retain table/source and subtract the same public
correction from each C_t. This remains a bijection for arbitrary nonlinear
mask-dependent builders, without any preimage correctness/shortness premise.
Thus the existing recursive-quadratic uniform-source ideal remains uniform
JOINTLY in the entire retained view. It is not a row-marginal argument.

Actual source simulation and computational bound
-------------------------------------------------
sourceSimulator is an explicit public map from the full ordinary transcript.
sourceSimulator_real proves its real output has the distribution of freshView.
sourceSimulator_uniform proves its uniform-source output is the FULL uniform
retained view, using the explicit inverse and the existing raw-view theorem.
Both statements include the common source, whole table and all controls.

keygen_public_evalDist links the stored materialized key generator to that
same sampler. oneTime_game_evalDist then equates its actual standard one-time
IND-CPA experiment to the materialized GSW game, including adaptive messages
chosen after the complete public view and the full ciphertext continuation.

sampled_game_abs_advantage_le and oneTime_abs_signedAdvantage_le prove the
ordinary-source bound for the explicit parameterized binary secret family.
Its right side is exactly the existing uniform-secret ordinaryReductionLoss
with the actual lifted public observer, implementationLoss, and subset-masking
loss. It has NO residual cubic-control or circular-security advantage term
for THIS materialized candidate. The ordinary losses themselves are explicit
finite reduction advantages, not assumed negligible in these theorems.

The builder is quantified as a mathematical function. A computational
negligibility theorem still requires a specified polynomial-time builder and
closure of all resulting reduction adversaries in the allowed class. The
new result does not make an exponential search efficient or assume that
every mathematical postprocessor preserves computational indistinguishability.

Correctness for the same sampler and shared error tape
------------------------------------------------------
freshDraw records one full private source draw, and freshView_eq_draw proves
that this is the draw used by the security sampler. For arbitrary source
masks, every binary secret and every supported scalar error tape, the new
freshDraw_control_error_bound proves simultaneously for each control t:

  |decrypt_s(C_t)-cubicPhase(A,u_t,s)|_q
      <= (n+1)*E + E*sum_{pair,column}|u_t[pair,column]|_q.

The first term is the ACTUAL error of that control's fresh recursive zero.
The second is the ACTUAL linear error of the common retained source, with
the compiler's public canonical coordinates. No independence of these errors
or of different controls is needed: the theorem is pointwise in the entire
draw and universally quantified over its control index. The claimed cubic
target still needs the genuine coefficient/preimage certificate.

The new smoke proof embeds the already saved n=3, width-six source and its
top-weight coefficient witness in one draw with two public table columns and
two copies of the top control. Both copies use the SAME six source errors;
their additional recursive-zero masks/errors may vary arbitrarily. For all
eight binary keys and every supported error tape with E=256, BOTH controls
have target (q/2)*s_0*s_1*s_2 and error at most 13,568:

  4*256 + 49*256 = 13,568.

This is a universal shared-source algebra/noise check, not a finite hardness
claim, a production-length public-table experiment, or evidence of a secure
family. Its source remains publicly recoverable at that radius.

2026-10-20 -- COMPLETE CONTROL TARGETS, SAMPLER SUPPORT AND REUSABLE EVALUATION
=========================================================================

The preceding goal turn was PROGRESS: it proved the actual joint ordinary-source

New sources and retained evidence
---------------------------------
  FormalProof4FHE/LWE/GSWLinearCharacterPolynomial.lean
  FormalProof4FHE/LWE/GSWLinearCharacterBootstrap.lean
  FormalProof4FHETest/GSWLinearCharacterBootstrapSmoke.lean
  scripts/check-gsw-character-complete-targets.py

A public discrepancy certificate instead of assumed cancellation
----------------------------------------------------------------
For arbitrary commutative rings, cubicPolynomial explicitly represents

  sum_{pair,r distinct from its endpoints}
    4*(A*u_pair)_r * X_first * X_second * X_r.

eval_cubicPolynomial proves that evaluating this actual public sparse
polynomial gives the compiler's actual cubicPhase. No sorted-triple relation
or cancellation is assumed. cubicPolynomial_three identifies the coefficient
with the already checked coefficient3 in the three-bit case.

For a polynomial P over ZMod q, define its public coefficientBound as

  sum_{monomial in support(P)} |centered(coeff(P,monomial))|.

Every monomial evaluates to zero or one on EVERY binary secret, so the new
eval_binary_bound proves |eval_s(P)|_q <= coefficientBound(P). The result
allows arbitrary monomial degrees and repeated variables. In particular,
cubic_discrepancy_bound bounds the real phase discrepancy by the coefficient
bound of cubicPolynomial minus an explicit public target polynomial. This is
a deterministic, publicly checkable error bound, not a security assumption,
secret-dependent acceptance oracle or probabilistic cancellation assertion.

Completing ALL original-bit gadget controls
-------------------------------------------
The existing controlPolynomial/piecePolynomial gives exactly the constant-
feature, linear-feature and repeated-index quadratic-feature messages on
binary keys. The new public polynomialCipher has phase equal to that fixed
quadratic polynomial. The completed control adds this fixed public ciphertext
to the already materialized character control and stores the entire GSW
matrix with storeOriginal. No private computation is deferred to evaluation.

The distinct-index cubic target is also an explicit public polynomial. The
controlMessage_split theorem proves that the quadratic message plus this
cubic target is EXACTLY the existing bootstrapper's required controlMessage,
with the actual finProdFinEquiv/featureEquiv column layout. Repeated indices
are handled by the existing proved Boolean identities, not by treating them
as independent cubic monomials.

For each packed control slot t and actual retained source mask A, the public
bound is now

  J_t(A) = E*sum|u_t(A)|_q
             + coefficientBound(cubicPolynomial(A,u_t)-targetPolynomial_t),
  K(A)   = (n+1)*E + max_t J_t(A).

The finite maximum uses Finset.sup and also handles an empty control layout.
The two lowest base-two gadget weights need no illegal division by four:
u=0 is permitted, with its actual omitted-target discrepancy charged to J_t.
targetPolynomial_bound proves that charge is at most base^level. With u=0,
all noncubic slots have zero compiler discrepancy and only their actual
fresh recursive-zero noise remains. The zero-source contribution is counted
once in K(A), in the same original-key ciphertext coordinates.

Actual key generation, publication and evaluator connection
------------------------------------------------------------
The new keygen eagerly returns the original public encryption table, the
ENTIRE preceding retained source/raw-control context, and all completed
StoredOriginal evaluator controls. It retains only the same original source
secret privately. Complete controls are deterministic public postprocessing
of that retained context; no new key or trapdoor is drawn.

binaryKeygen_eq_draw proves the exact sampler identity for one binary key,
one full mask tape and one finite IID scalar-error tape. keygen_noiseBounds
then derives, for EVERY supported key-generator output, an embedded binary
private key, the real public-table phase bound (n+1)*E and the real completed
control bound K(A), using only the scalar sampler's supported error bound.
This discharges the actual source-error/support obligation rather than
postulating that the generated controls are already correct.

noiseBound_bootstrap feeds those actual controls and their computed K(A)
into the existing same-key refresh theorem. decode_evaluate_from_source
connects them to ARBITRARY finite shared NAND programs, without a circuit-
depth-dependent key chain. Its remaining hypotheses are the explicit input,
rounding, output and decoding margins in terms of K(A). There is no separate
correct-control premise or new error-independence hypothesis.

The exact oneTime_game_eq and oneTime_signedAdvantage_eq retain all of the
old context beside the completed stored controls and connect this scheme's
standard IND-CPA game to GSWLinearCharacterSecurity's actual ordinary-LWE
bound through an explicit public adversary adapter. Publishing the completed
controls therefore introduces no new cubic/circular advantage term for this
candidate. Computational negligibility still needs polynomial-time builder
and reduction-class closure; the equalities do not justify unbounded search.

Lean integration checks and scope
---------------------------------

at q=2^64 and E=256, in the complete n=3 original-bit layout with 2,496 scalar
control slots. The saved top cubic has compiler bound at most 12,544 and actual
completed-control error at most 13,568 including its recursive-zero error.
The theorem is uniform over all binary keys, all supported shared error tapes
and arbitrary remaining source masks. Separate universal smoke theorems cover
every noncubic feature and both low gadget levels.

Its builder deliberately supplies the saved coefficient only to genuine

storage and error accounting; it is NOT a whole-budget generator. In
particular its uncompiled intermediate cubic levels are not claimed to meet
the refresh margins. Nor is this recoverable source claimed hard.

Finite complete-target replay without another search
-----------------------------------------------------
The new standard-library checker reads the HASH-PINNED previous artifact.
It independently reconstructs EVERY cubic coefficient over the original
modulus, checks all 1,936 saved exact preimages, and chooses either a saved
character coefficient or the ZERO correction at each triple/level according
to the smaller public bound. It uses no quadratic approximation or nonlinear
body baseline outside the newly formalized class. It then covers every
constant, linear and ordered-quadratic feature for every original bit and
gadget level, including all repeated-index cases.

The replay covers all 31,344 complete control slots in the four fixed cases:

  n  block_m  bits  controls  public_columns  ordinary_columns  max J         4F gate
  3     1      64     2496          977             13895           109312     fail
  4     1      68     5712         1581             36468         80485376     fail
  6     1      80    20640         3617            169802  247122892628480     fail

The source counts are M+(public_columns+controls)*(n+1), with M=3*block_m.
The passing case has 2,112 exact lower-degree targets, 300 exact cubic

Its complete K=15,104 passes the conservative gate and the ACTUAL rounding
and decoding inequalities for output bound

  lookup*rows*growth*(growth+1)*K.

The fresh-encryption bound at all 977 public-table columns is also checked

is insecure for its entire supported error set. There are ZERO passes

This is a public coefficient/target replay, not a sampled joint production
view, raw-coordinate byte fixture or secure asymptotic family. The semantic

replay does not claim that the Lean kernel checked all numeric preimages
in this JSON. The source-distribution success/shortness theorem remains
unproved. No masks were resampled and no lattice/SMT search was rerun.

2026-10-20 -- EXACT TARGET-PAIR SHORTCUT: PUBLIC KEY LISTS WITH THE TORSION GUARD
=============================================================================

The preceding goal turn was PROGRESS: it connected every control target and
the actual source sampler to stored reusable evaluation. This continuation
audits a possible faster coefficient generator, rather than adding another
conditional positive security wrapper. The full goal remains ACTIVE and
unfinished. No earlier search/build job was live at the start.

The potential shortcut and its actual coefficient equations
------------------------------------------------------------
For a target triple I={i,j,k}, restrict the character coefficients to the
three pairs inside I, rather than all n*(n-1)/2 pairs. There are then 3*M
unknowns for joined source width M, and only 1+3*(n-3) potentially nonzero
distinct-cubic equations. This reduces the lattice dimension substantially.

The relevant public certificate, after putting the target coordinates first,
is

  4*coefficient3(A_head,u) = weight               (mod q),
  4*(A*u_pair)_r = 0 for every outside row r     (mod q).

For each outside triple containing one of the selected pairs, there is only
that one pair's contribution, so these outside equations are necessary for
the EXACT target-pair subfamily. All other cubic coefficients vanish by
support. The new target_pair_cubic_phase theorem proves that this certificate
gives the actual full ambient cubic phase weight*s_i*s_j*s_k; it does not
assume unproved cancellation or silently omit outside secret coordinates.

The four factors CANNOT be canceled at this modulus. Solving modulo q/4
gives 4*A*u=0 outside the triple, not A*u=0 modulo q. Consequently the original
compiler may have genuine quadratic/linear terms involving outside secret
coordinates even under an exact cubic certificate. The first unscaled
three-bit locality idea would therefore be wrong.

A valid public local test after scaling
---------------------------------------
Scale the PUBLIC coefficients by four and form the three-dimensional
character polynomial using the target rows A_head and the ORIGINAL full
source bodies y. This polynomial is publicly computable; its definition
does not receive the remaining secret or source errors. Its projections now
annihilate all outside rows modulo q. The confined_projection and
compileCanonical_eq_of_projections theorems prove that its phase is the same
as if those bodies came from A_head and the actual source errors.

local_compiler_scaled_error_bound proves that, for every binary target key,
every remaining source secret and every supported error vector,

  |4*weight*s_i*s_j*s_k - localQuadratic(s_i,s_j,s_k)|_q
      <= 4*E*sum|u_pair,column|_q.

The compiler is linear in these public coefficients; the scale and coefficient
norm inequalities are proved explicitly. This is a real-source statement,
not a guessed-key residual assertion. Adding dense recursive zeros to the
published controls does not conceal this separate public test when the
retained source and public coefficient builder are available: it is computed
directly from that source, before rerandomization.

Scaling ALSO changes the target weight. At the top base-two gadget weight,
4*(q/2)=0, so a top-weight key-list argument would fail. The correct choice
for q=2^levels, levels>=4, is weight=2^(levels-4), where the scaled weight
has centered magnitude q/4. scaled_lower_weight_magnitude and
scaled_lower_weight_gap prove that

  128*J < q  ==>  8*(4*J) < |4*weight|_q.

scaled_gap_of_control_budget obtains the same inequality from the existing
conservative gate 4*F*K<q whenever J<=K and F>=32. Thus this test uses a real
lower gadget level with the same whole-table budget scale; it does not
silently assume that an annihilated top target stays separated.

Pointwise candidate-key and prefix bounds
-----------------------------------------
New source: FormalProof4FHE/LWE/GSWTripleLocalCompiler.lean.

The existing eight-corner difference theorem says that a quadratic cannot
fit all eight assignments to a nonzero separated cubic within bound B if
8*B is below the target magnitude. A family of keys fitting a public local
quadratic constraint on EVERY triple therefore shatters no triple. The new
proofs use Sauer--Shelah to establish

  compatible_keys <= 1+n+choose(n,2).

Weights and coefficients may depend arbitrarily on the entire fixed public
transcript. No independence of coefficients, keys, different targets or
their errors is assumed. sorted_candidate_key_bound requires only one
constraint per unordered triple, not six copies in different feature orders.
scaled_candidate_key_bound connects the scaled lower-level error threshold
directly to this candidate bound.

prefix_candidate_key_bound is also proved. For any public set of assigned
coordinates, all partial assignments are false outside that set, and only
triples already contained in it need constraints. The candidate family at
each stage still has the polynomial bound. This addresses the algorithmic
distinction between a small final set and an efficient way to enumerate it.

The executable public enumeration grows each prefix by one bit and checks
only newly completed triples. With the candidate bound, its number of local
constraint evaluations is bounded by

  2*n*(1+n+choose(n,2))*choose(n,2),

and each local quadratic has constant many coefficients of modulus word
length. The Python implementation receives only the public constraints;
it never receives the planted key. This is an implemented candidate-list
procedure supported by the formal prefix bound, not a Lean proof of the
algorithm's full correctness or membership in a formal PPT adversary class.

Finite checks, sharpness and the nonzero-outside example
-------------------------------------------------------
  scripts/check-gsw-triple-local-keys.py
  docs/GSWTripleLocalKeyExploration.json
  FormalProof4FHETest/GSWTripleLocalSmoke.lean

Fifteen deterministic synthetic local-constraint fixtures cover n=0 through
64. Nine have independently enumerated exhaustive small-dimensional
solutions. The other larger checks use the public prefix algorithm; none
attempt an exponential full-key enumeration. Eight saturating fixtures use
the all-zero quadratic, for which the surviving keys have Hamming weight
at most two. Their candidate counts exactly equal 1+n+choose(n,2), reaching
529 at n=32. Seven planted fixtures use public quadratic interpolation of
seven corners, excluding a different corner from the planted assignment.
Every resulting public accept table is checked at all eight assignments.

The algorithm checks 3,086,343 public local constraints in total. All final
sets contain the diagnostic real key. Four separately sampled validation
rows per fixture leave one candidate in these tests. These local constraint
fixtures are SYNTHETIC: they are not claimed to be generated by an ordinary
LWE challenge, and the validation tests are not a formal reduction or an
asymptotic distinguishing-advantage theorem. Fixture generation may know the
diagnostic key; neither public enumeration nor candidate validation does.

The separate actual source fixture uses q=1024, identity coefficient rows,
head matrix entry A[2,0]=16, and outside row [256,512,768]. It has
4*A*u=0 outside the triple but A*u!=0. Its exact cubic weight is 64. The
full ambient compiler has supported error at most 3 at E=1, and ALL 1,296
ambient phase checks pass. The naive UNSCALED local quadratic has error
512 at key 000 and tail 1 with ZERO source error, and fails its proposed
bound in 432 cases. This is a failure of the omitted-outside locality
argument, not a correctness failure of the full character compiler.

All 1,296 scaled local source-phase checks pass the actual bound 12. The
scaled target is 256 and has the strict eight-corner margin; the scaled top
weight 512 vanishes. The Lean smoke module checks the public torsion and
target certificates, the full ambient cubic identity, that explicit
unscaled failure, and the scaled error theorem for ALL target binary keys,
EVERY remaining source secret and EVERY supported error vector. The numeric
certificates use kernel decide, with no native_decide trust extension.

Scope and remaining generator requirement
------------------------------------------
This is a pointwise public candidate-key weakness of the EXACT target-pair
shortcut, with an explicit enumeration procedure. A full formal ordinary-LWE
attack would still have to connect the public generator's success event,
row relabeling, independent validation samples, probability losses and
allowed-adversary complexity class. Those claims are not silently included
in the candidate-cardinality theorem. The existing full-security goal's
PPT reduction closure is still unproved.

The generic dense character compiler is NOT triple-local and has no such
outside-projection condition. No small-key-list conclusion for it follows
from these theorems. The source bridge here also assumes exact cubic target
equations; it does not rule out arbitrary approximate equations or other
nonlocal generators. Private coefficients correlated with key generation
and unavailable from the public view are outside this public-builder filter.
No general impossibility theorem for same-key FHE is asserted.

The next generator search must not claim that shrinking to these three pairs
is a free complexity improvement. The dense/other nonlocal family shortness
and success-probability problem remains unresolved. A verified efficient
generator meeting the ACTUAL asymptotic whole-table budget is still missing;
this result does not replace that requirement with a key-list hypothesis.

Fresh primary-source searches returned a previously audited layered-key QFHE
claim. The existing full-paper audit earlier in this log already addresses
its key schedule. It is not a new same-secret construction, and no new
reference was downloaded or added. No lattice or SMT search was rerun.
